        size = source.stat().st_size
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ImportPreflightError(
            ImportIssue(
                code="XLSX_FILE_UNREADABLE",
                message="XLSX 文件不可读取。",
            )
        ) from exc
    if size <= 0:
        _reject("XLSX_EMPTY_FILE", "XLSX 文件为空。")
    if size > limits.max_file_bytes:
        _reject("XLSX_FILE_TOO_LARGE", "XLSX 文件超过大小限制。")

    try:
        raw_file = source.read_bytes()
    except OSError as exc:
        raise ImportPreflightError(
            ImportIssue(
                code="XLSX_FILE_UNREADABLE",
                message="XLSX 文件不可读取。",
            )
        ) from exc
    if len(raw_file) > limits.max_file_bytes:
        _reject("XLSX_FILE_TOO_LARGE", "XLSX 文件超过大小限制。")
    content_sha256 = hashlib.sha256(raw_file).hexdigest()
    try:
        archive = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(raw_file))
    except (OSError, zipfile.BadZipFile) as exc:
        raise ImportPreflightError(
            ImportIssue(
                code="XLSX_ZIP_INVALID",
                message="文件不是有效的 XLSX ZIP 包。",
            )
        ) from exc

    with archive:
        members = _inspect_archive(archive, limits=limits)
        content_types = _read_entry(
            archive,
            members,
            "[Content_Types].xml",
        )
        if _has_referenced_macro_content_type(content_types, members):
            _reject("XLSX_ACTIVE_CONTENT", "XLSX 不允许宏启用内容。")
        worksheets = _worksheet_parts(archive, members)
        shared = _shared_strings(archive, members)
        all_rows: list[tuple[int, dict[int, Any]]] | None = None
        selected_sheet: tuple[str, str] | None = None
        for sheet in worksheets:
            payload = _read_entry(archive, members, sheet[1])
            rows = _read_sheet_rows(
                payload,
                shared=shared,
                limits=limits,
                location=sheet[1],
            )
            if all_rows is None:
                all_rows = rows
                selected_sheet = sheet

        assert all_rows is not None and selected_sheet is not None
        if not all_rows:
            _reject("XLSX_HEADER_REQUIRED", "工作表没有表头。")
        header_row_number, header_values = all_rows[0]
        if not header_values:
            _reject("XLSX_HEADER_REQUIRED", "工作表首行没有表头。")
        last_column = max(
            (max(row_values, default=0) for _, row_values in all_rows),
            default=0,
        )
        raw_headers = [
            header_values.get(column_number, "")
            for column_number in range(1, last_column + 1)
        ]
        columns = _column_plan(raw_headers)

    return LoadedTable(
        content_sha256=content_sha256,
        sheet_name=selected_sheet[0],
        header_row_number=header_row_number,
        columns=columns,
        last_column=last_column,
        rows=all_rows,
    )


def read_xlsx_table(
    path: str | Path,
    *,
    limits: XlsxLimits | None = None,
) -> LoadedTable:
    """按与预检相同的安全规则读取整张表，供导入侧取全部行。"""

    return _load_table(Path(path), limits=limits or XlsxLimits())


def preflight_xlsx(
    path: str | Path,
    *,
    target: ImportTarget | None = None,
    limits: XlsxLimits | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Return a deterministic, non-persistent import plan for one XLSX."""

    source = Path(path)
    target = target or ImportTarget()
    limits = limits or XlsxLimits()
    _assert_target_is_preflightable(target)
    table = _load_table(source, limits=limits)
    content_sha256 = table.content_sha256
    header_row_number = table.header_row_number
    columns = table.columns
    all_rows = table.rows
    last_column = table.last_column
    mappings, conflicts = _mapping_preview(columns)
    preview_rows: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for source_row, row_values in all_rows[1 : limits.preview_rows + 1]:
        raw_cells = [
            row_values.get(column_number, "")
            for column_number in range(1, last_column + 1)
        ]
        preview_rows.append(
            {
                "source_row": source_row,
                "raw_cells": raw_cells,
                "values_by_internal_name": {
                    column["internal_name"]: raw_cells[index]
                    for index, column in enumerate(columns)
                },
            }
        )

    idempotency_material = {
        "schema_version": PREFLIGHT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "content_sha256": content_sha256,
        "domain": target.domain.casefold(),
        "target_kind": target.target_kind,
        "sheet_name": table.sheet_name,
        "columns": columns,
    }
    batch_key = "p0e:" + hashlib.sha256(
        _canonical_json(idempotency_material).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    return {
        "schema_version": PREFLIGHT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
        "mode": "preflight_only",
        "writes_business_database": False,
        "source_file": source.name,
        "content_sha256": content_sha256,
        "batch_key": batch_key,
        "domain": target.domain,
        "target_kind": target.target_kind,
        "sheet": {
            "name": table.sheet_name,
            "header_row": header_row_number,
            "data_row_count": max(0, len(all_rows) - 1),
            "preview_row_count": len(preview_rows),
        },
        "columns": columns,
        "mapping_preview": mappings,
        "mapping_conflicts": conflicts,
        "applied_mapping": {},
        "raw_preview": preview_rows,
        "requires_human_confirmation": bool(mappings),
    }

===== FILE backend/app/paid_acceptance_guard.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.dialects.mysql import insert as mysql_insert
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as postgresql_insert
from sqlalchemy.dialects.sqlite import insert as sqlite_insert
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database import reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction
from .models import (
    PaidAcceptanceCallReservation,
    PaidAcceptanceCampaign,
)


_CAMPAIGN_ID = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,119}")
_ATTEMPT_KEY = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:/-]{0,239}")
_CORRELATION_ID = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:/-]{0,199}")


class PaidAcceptanceGuardError(RuntimeError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CallReservation:
    reservation_id: int
    campaign_id: str
    attempt_key: str
    ordinal: int
    call_limit: int
    status: str
    reused: bool


def _validate_reservation_input(
    campaign_id: str, attempt_key: str, limit: int
) -> tuple[str, str]:
    normalized_campaign = str(campaign_id or "").strip()
    normalized_attempt = str(attempt_key or "").strip()
    if _CAMPAIGN_ID.fullmatch(normalized_campaign) is None:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_campaign_id_invalid", "付费验收批次标识无效"
        )
    if _ATTEMPT_KEY.fullmatch(normalized_attempt) is None:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_attempt_key_invalid", "付费调用 attempt 标识无效"
        )
    if (
        not isinstance(limit, int)
        or isinstance(limit, bool)
        or limit < 1
        or limit > 1_000_000
    ):
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_call_limit_invalid", "付费验收调用上限无效"
        )
    return normalized_campaign, normalized_attempt


def _ensure_campaign(
    db: Session, *, campaign_id: str, limit: int
) -> PaidAcceptanceCampaign:
    dialect = db.get_bind().dialect.name
    values = {
        "campaign_id": campaign_id,
        "call_limit": limit,
        "reserved_count": 0,
    }
    if dialect == "sqlite":
        statement = sqlite_insert(PaidAcceptanceCampaign).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_conflict_do_nothing(
            index_elements=["campaign_id"]
        )
    elif dialect == "mysql":
        statement = mysql_insert(PaidAcceptanceCampaign).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_duplicate_key_update(
            campaign_id=statement.inserted.campaign_id
        )
    elif dialect == "postgresql":
        statement = postgresql_insert(PaidAcceptanceCampaign).values(**values)
        statement = statement.on_conflict_do_nothing(
            index_elements=["campaign_id"]
        )
    else:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_database_unsupported", "当前数据库不支持付费验收门禁"
        )
    db.execute(statement)
    campaign = db.scalar(
        select(PaidAcceptanceCampaign)
        .where(PaidAcceptanceCampaign.campaign_id == campaign_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if campaign is None:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_campaign_missing", "付费验收批次初始化失败"
        )
    if campaign.call_limit != limit:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_call_limit_conflict", "同一付费验收批次不能变更调用上限"
        )
    return campaign


def _reservation_snapshot(
    row: PaidAcceptanceCallReservation,
    campaign: PaidAcceptanceCampaign,
    *,
    reused: bool,
) -> CallReservation:
    if row.id is None:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_reservation_missing", "付费调用预留未持久化"
        )
    return CallReservation(
        reservation_id=row.id,
        campaign_id=campaign.campaign_id,
        attempt_key=row.attempt_key,
        ordinal=row.ordinal,
        call_limit=campaign.call_limit,
        status=row.status,
        reused=reused,
    )


def reserve_acceptance_call(
    db: Session,
    *,
    campaign_id: str,
    attempt_key: str,
    limit: int,
) -> CallReservation:
    normalized_campaign, normalized_attempt = _validate_reservation_input(
        campaign_id, attempt_key, limit
    )
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    campaign = _ensure_campaign(
        db, campaign_id=normalized_campaign, limit=limit
    )
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(PaidAcceptanceCallReservation).where(
            PaidAcceptanceCallReservation.campaign_db_id == campaign.id,
            PaidAcceptanceCallReservation.attempt_key == normalized_attempt,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return _reservation_snapshot(existing, campaign, reused=True)
    if campaign.reserved_count >= campaign.call_limit:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_call_limit_exhausted",
            "本轮付费验收已达到调用上限，未发起模型请求",
        )
    ordinal = campaign.reserved_count + 1
    campaign.reserved_count = ordinal
    campaign.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    row = PaidAcceptanceCallReservation(
        campaign_db_id=campaign.id,
        attempt_key=normalized_attempt,
        ordinal=ordinal,
        status="reserved",
    )
    db.add(row)
    db.flush()
    return _reservation_snapshot(row, campaign, reused=False)


def settle_acceptance_call(
    db: Session,
    *,
    reservation_id: int,
    provider_correlation_id: str | None,
    outcome: str,
) -> CallReservation:
    if outcome not in {"succeeded", "failed"}:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_outcome_invalid", "付费调用结算状态无效"
        )
    correlation = (
        str(provider_correlation_id).strip()
        if provider_correlation_id is not None
        else None
    )
    if correlation is not None and _CORRELATION_ID.fullmatch(correlation) is None:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_correlation_invalid", "供应商调用标识无效或过长"
        )
    reserve_sqlite_immediate_transaction(db)
    row = db.scalar(
        select(PaidAcceptanceCallReservation)
        .where(PaidAcceptanceCallReservation.id == reservation_id)
        .with_for_update()
    )
    if row is None:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_reservation_missing", "付费调用预留不存在"
        )
    campaign = db.get(PaidAcceptanceCampaign, row.campaign_db_id)
    if campaign is None:
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_campaign_missing", "付费验收批次不存在"
        )
    if row.status in {"succeeded", "failed"}:
        if (
            row.status == outcome
            and row.provider_correlation_id == correlation
        ):
            return _reservation_snapshot(row, campaign, reused=True)
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_settlement_conflict", "付费调用已使用不同结果结算"
        )
    if row.status != "reserved":
        raise PaidAcceptanceGuardError(
            "acceptance_settlement_conflict", "付费调用当前状态不能结算"
        )
    row.status = outcome
    row.provider_correlation_id = correlation
    row.settled_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    db.flush()
    return _reservation_snapshot(row, campaign, reused=False)

===== FILE backend/app/personal_mcp.py =====
"""Personal MCP credentials. Only hashes are persisted; no shared machine token."""
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import hmac
import re
import secrets

from fastapi import HTTPException
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, DateTime, ForeignKey, String, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column

from .database import Base


class PersonalMcpCredential(Base):
    __tablename__ = 'personal_mcp_credentials'
    user_id: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(ForeignKey('users.id', ondelete='CASCADE'), primary_key=True)
    token_hash: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(String(64))
    enabled: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(Boolean, default=True)
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    rotated_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), default=lambda: datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    last_used_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(DateTime(timezone=True), nullable=True)
    last_tool: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(String(100), nullable=True)


def digest(token):
    return hashlib.sha256(token.encode()).hexdigest()


def authenticate(db, token, *, lock=False):
    from .models import User
    match = re.fullmatch(r'mcp_([1-9][0-9]{0,15})_([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43})', token or '')
    if not match:
        raise HTTPException(401, 'MCP令牌无效', headers={'WWW-Authenticate': 'Bearer'})
    user_id = int(match[1])
    query = select(User).where(User.id == user_id)
    if lock:
        query = query.with_for_update()
    user = db.scalar(query.execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    query = select(PersonalMcpCredential).where(PersonalMcpCredential.user_id == user_id)
    if lock:
        query = query.with_for_update()
    credential = db.scalar(query.execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if not user or not user.is_active or not credential or not credential.enabled or not hmac.compare_digest(credential.token_hash, digest(token)):
        raise HTTPException(401, 'MCP已停用、令牌失效或账号不可用', headers={'WWW-Authenticate': 'Bearer'})
    return user, credential


def rotate(db, user):
    from .authz import lock_access_users
    from .models import User
    lock_access_users(db, [user.id])
    live = db.get(User, user.id, populate_existing=True)
    if not live or not live.is_active:
        raise HTTPException(403, '账号不可用')
    row = db.get(PersonalMcpCredential, user.id)
    token = f'mcp_{user.id}_{secrets.token_urlsafe(32)}'
    if row is None:
        row = PersonalMcpCredential(user_id=user.id)
        db.add(row)
    row.token_hash = digest(token)
    row.enabled = True
    row.rotated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    from .audit import append_audit_event
    append_audit_event(db,category='personal_mcp_credentials',action='rotate',subject_type='user',
        subject_id=user.id,actor=f'user:{user.id}',payload={'enabled':True})
    db.commit()
    return token


def disable(db, user):
    from .authz import lock_access_users
    lock_access_users(db, [user.id])
    row = db.get(PersonalMcpCredential, user.id, populate_existing=True)
    if row:
        row.enabled = False
        from .audit import append_audit_event
        append_audit_event(db,category='personal_mcp_credentials',action='disable',subject_type='user',
            subject_id=user.id,actor=f'user:{user.id}',payload={'enabled':False})
        db.commit()


def status(db, user):
    row = db.get(PersonalMcpCredential, user.id)
    return {'enabled': bool(row and row.enabled), 'rotated_at': row.rotated_at if row else None,
            'last_used_at': row.last_used_at if row else None, 'last_tool': row.last_tool if row else None,
            'transport': 'streamable-http', 'path': '/api/mcp',
            'production_write': False, 'incremental_switch_write': False}

===== FILE backend/app/personal_mcp_api.py =====
"""Stateless Streamable HTTP MCP, protocol 2025-06-18; no public proxy tool."""
from contextlib import nullcontext
from datetime import datetime, timezone
import hashlib
import json
import uuid

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.encoders import jsonable_encoder
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, Response
from pydantic import ValidationError
from starlette.concurrency import run_in_threadpool
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .database import get_db
from .personal_mcp import authenticate, disable, rotate, status
from .personal_mcp_tools import TOOLS, invoke, schema
from .authz import has_permission, protect_write_transaction
from .audit import append_audit_event
from .prompt_lab import LabWriteLock, PromptLabError
from .baseline_truth import BaselineTruthError

PROTOCOL = '2025-06-18'
MAX_BODY = 1_048_576


def rpc_error(ident,code,message):
    return {'jsonrpc':'2.0','id':ident,'error':{'code':code,'message':message}}


def dispatch(db,token,message):
    from .employee_lifecycle import check_employee_access
    user,_ = authenticate(db,token)
    check_employee_access(db,user)
    db.rollback()  # no old snapshot before LabWriteLock
    method=message['method']; ident=message.get('id'); params=message.get('params')
    if params is None: params={}
    if not isinstance(params,dict): return rpc_error(ident,-32602,'params必须是对象')
    if method=='initialize':
        return {'jsonrpc':'2.0','id':ident,'result':{'protocolVersion':PROTOCOL,'capabilities':{'tools':{'listChanged':False}},'serverInfo':{'name':'label-platform','version':'1.0.0'},'instructions':'仅存量实验操作；继承个人权限。人工确认必须明确，不能操作增量开关、生产绑定或业务投递。'}}
    if method.startswith('notifications/'):
        return None
    if method=='ping': return {'jsonrpc':'2.0','id':ident,'result':{}}
    if method=='tools/list':
        if params.get('cursor'): return rpc_error(ident,-32602,'无效分页游标')
        user,_=authenticate(db,token)
        tools=[{'name':name,'description':t.description,'inputSchema':schema(t),'annotations':{'readOnlyHint':not t.write,'destructiveHint':t.write,'openWorldHint':name=='start_regression'}} for name,t in TOOLS.items() if has_permission(user,t.permission)]
        return {'jsonrpc':'2.0','id':ident,'result':{'tools':tools}}
    if method!='tools/call': return rpc_error(ident,-32601,'不支持的方法')
    name=params.get('name'); arguments=params.get('arguments',{})
    if not isinstance(name,str) or name not in TOOLS or not isinstance(arguments,dict):
        return rpc_error(ident,-32602,'工具名称或参数无效')
    tool=TOOLS[name]
    try:
        with LabWriteLock(db) if tool.write else nullcontext():
            user,credential=authenticate(db,token,lock=tool.write)
            if tool.write:
                protect_write_transaction(db,user)
            result=invoke(db,user,name,arguments)
            result=jsonable_encoder(result)
            if not tool.write:
                # Finish the read snapshot before taking the short credential lock.
                db.rollback()
                from .authz import lock_access_users
                lock_access_users(db,[user.id])
                user,credential=authenticate(db,token,lock=True)
            credential.last_used_at=datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            credential.last_tool=name
            if tool.write:
                ids={k:v for k,v in result.items() if k in {'id','run_id','baseline_set_id','workflow_version_id','asset_id','asset_key','revision_id','draft_key','revision','canonical_hash'} and isinstance(v,(str,int))} if isinstance(result,dict) else {}
                run_id=result.get('id') if name=='start_regression' and isinstance(result,dict) else None
                append_audit_event(db,event_key='mcp:'+uuid.uuid4().hex,category='personal_mcp',action=name,
                    subject_type='baseline_regression_run' if run_id else 'user',subject_id=run_id or user.id,
                    actor='mcp:'+str(user.id),payload={'arguments_sha256':hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(arguments,sort_keys=True,ensure_ascii=False).encode()).hexdigest(),'permission_revision':user.permission_revision,'objects':ids})
            db.commit()
        if name=='read_asset_image':
            return {'jsonrpc':'2.0','id':ident,'result':{'content':[{'type':'image',**result}],'isError':False}}
        return {'jsonrpc':'2.0','id':ident,'result':{'content':[{'type':'text','text':json.dumps(result,ensure_ascii=False)}],'structuredContent':result if isinstance(result,dict) else {'items':result},'isError':False}}
    except (ValidationError,PromptLabError,BaselineTruthError,HTTPException) as exc:
        db.rollback()
        if isinstance(exc,HTTPException) and exc.status_code==401: raise
        if isinstance(exc,ValidationError): message='参数校验失败，请按工具Schema检查字段、类型及长度'
        elif isinstance(exc,HTTPException): message=str(exc.detail)
        else: message=f'{exc.code}: {exc}'
        return {'jsonrpc':'2.0','id':ident,'result':{'content':[{'type':'text','text':message}],'isError':True}}
    except Exception:
        db.rollback()
        # No exception bodies, prompts, endpoints or credentials returned to clients.
        message='操作未完成，请先查询原请求状态；不要更换幂等编号重复提交。' if tool.write else '读取失败，请稍后重试或在中台查看。'
        return {'jsonrpc':'2.0','id':ident,'result':{'content':[{'type':'text','text':message}],'isError':True}}


def build_personal_mcp_router(current_user):
    router=APIRouter()

    @router.get('/api/me/mcp')
    def get_status(user=Depends(current_user),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        return status(db,user)

    @router.post('/api/me/mcp/rotate')
    def issue(user=Depends(current_user),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        token=rotate(db,user)
        return JSONResponse({'token':token,'path':'/api/mcp','transport':'streamable-http'},headers={'Cache-Control':'no-store'})

    @router.delete('/api/me/mcp')
    def revoke(user=Depends(current_user),db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        disable(db,user)
        return {'enabled':False}

    @router.get('/api/mcp')
    def no_sse():
        return Response(status_code=405,headers={'Allow':'POST'})

    @router.post('/api/mcp')
    async def mcp(request:Request,db:Session=Depends(get_db)):
        # Native clients do not send Origin. Browser callers are deliberately not supported.
        if request.headers.get('origin'):
            raise HTTPException(403,'MCP仅接受原生客户端连接')
        authorization=request.headers.get('authorization','')
        if not authorization.lower().startswith('bearer '): raise HTTPException(401,'需要个人MCP令牌',headers={'WWW-Authenticate':'Bearer'})
        if request.headers.get('mcp-protocol-version',PROTOCOL)!=PROTOCOL:
            raise HTTPException(400,'不支持的MCP协议版本')
        if 'application/json' not in request.headers.get('content-type',''):
            raise HTTPException(415,'需要application/json')
        accept=request.headers.get('accept','')
        if 'application/json' not in accept or 'text/event-stream' not in accept:
            raise HTTPException(406,'Accept必须包含application/json和text/event-stream')
        chunks=[]; size=0
        async for chunk in request.stream():
            size+=len(chunk)
            if size>MAX_BODY: raise HTTPException(413,'请求过大')
            chunks.append(chunk)
        try: message=json.loads(b''.join(chunks))
        except (ValueError,UnicodeError): return JSONResponse(rpc_error(None,-32700,'JSON解析失败'),status_code=400)
        if not isinstance(message,dict) or message.get('jsonrpc')!='2.0' or not isinstance(message.get('method'),str) or ('id' in message and (isinstance(message['id'],bool) or not isinstance(message['id'],(int,str)))):
            return JSONResponse(rpc_error(None,-32600,'无效JSON-RPC请求'),status_code=400)
        if 'id' not in message and not message['method'].startswith('notifications/'):
            return JSONResponse(rpc_error(None,-32600,'工具调用必须带请求编号'),status_code=400)
        result=await run_in_threadpool(dispatch,db,authorization[7:],message)
        if result is None: return Response(status_code=202)
        return JSONResponse(result,headers={'Cache-Control':'no-store'})

    return router

===== FILE backend/app/personal_mcp_tools.py =====
"""Explicit stock-only tool allowlist, sharing existing service invariants."""
from dataclasses import dataclass
import json
import hashlib
from typing import Any

from fastapi import HTTPException
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, create_model
from sqlalchemy import select, func

from . import prompt_lab as lab, stock_lab as stock
from .authz import has_permission, require_categories
from .prompt_lab_api import CandidateRequest, RunRequest, GUIDE
from .stock_lab_api import (DraftRequest, FreezeRequest, SampleCloneRequest, TruthRequest,
                            ControlRequest, ComponentRequest, ImportRequest, check_key)


class Empty(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra='forbid', strict=True)


class Page(Empty):
    limit: int = Field(default=30, ge=1, le=100)
    before_id: int | None = Field(default=None, ge=1)


class Runs(Empty):
    limit: int = Field(default=30, ge=1, le=200)
    lab_only: bool = False


def args(name, base=Empty, **extra):
    return create_model(name, __base__=base, **extra)


ID = (int, Field(gt=0))
KEY = (str, Field(pattern=r'^[A-Za-z0-9.-]{1,80}$'))
McpRunRequest = args('McpRunRequest', RunRequest,
    client_request_id=(str, Field(min_length=8, max_length=80, pattern=r'^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+$')))


def personal_key(user_id, key):
    # Keep service keys bounded even when the caller supplies the maximum length.
    return f'mcp{user_id}.' + hashlib.sha256(key.encode()).hexdigest()[:48]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Tool:
    description: str
    model: type[BaseModel]
    permission: str
    write: bool = False


TOOLS = {
    'catalog': Tool('读取类目、模型、提示词、规则和组件目录；不返回模型地址或凭据。', Empty, 'workflows:read'),
    'read_asset_image': Tool('读取已授权存量素材图片（最多10MiB）；不能读取任意路径或URL。', args('AssetImage',asset_id=ID), 'assets:read'),
    'platform_capabilities': Tool('读取当前个人权限及存量工具边界，不开放生产绑定、增量开关或业务投递。', Empty, 'workflows:read'),
    'stock_guide': Tool('读取现有存量API使用说明。MCP使用个人令牌，说明中的机器令牌认证不适用。', Empty, 'workflows:read'),
    'list_mechanisms': Tool('分页列出冻结机制；scan_capped时必须继续翻页。', Page, 'workflows:read'),
    'get_mechanism': Tool('读取机制节点、提示词与输出合同。', args('Mechanism', version_id=ID), 'workflows:read'),
    'get_manifest': Tool('读取独立草稿的源工作流清单。', args('Manifest', version_id=ID), 'workflows:read'),
    'create_candidate': Tool('创建不可变提示词或输出Schema候选，不修改线上绑定。', CandidateRequest, 'prompts:write', True),
    'list_sample_sets': Tool('列出存量样本集。', Empty, 'assets:read'),
    'list_sample_items': Tool('分页读取样本和人工参考答案。', args('SampleItems', set_id=ID, offset=(int,Field(default=0,ge=0)),limit=(int,Field(default=100,ge=1,le=500))), 'assets:read'),
    'get_reference': Tool('读取单条人工参考答案及版本哈希。', args('Reference', set_id=ID,item_id=ID), 'reviews:read'),
    'confirm_reference': Tool('明确确认后追加人工参考答案版本；保留历史。', args('ConfirmReference',TruthRequest,set_id=ID,item_id=ID), 'reviews:write',True),
    'clone_samples': Tool('复制选定样本成为独立样本集。', SampleCloneRequest,'reviews:write',True),
    'import_samples': Tool('从已有素材ID导入独立存量样本集。',ImportRequest,'reviews:write',True),
    'start_regression': Tool('发起真实模型存量回归，会产生用量；必须提供稳定的client_request_id，重试沿用原编号。',McpRunRequest,'regressions:run',True),
    'list_regressions': Tool('列出存量回归轮次。',Runs,'reviews:read'),
    'get_regression': Tool('读取存量回归详情。',args('Regression',run_id=ID),'reviews:read'),
    'regression_items': Tool('分页读取存量结果。',args('RegressionItems',run_id=ID,offset=(int,Field(default=0,ge=0)),limit=(int,Field(default=100,ge=1,le=500))),'reviews:read'),
    'cancel_regression': Tool('取消实验室存量回归，保留已有结果。',args('CancelRegression',run_id=ID),'regressions:run',True),
    'control_regression_items': Tool('暂停、继续、重试或取消指定存量条目；不能控制增量。',args('ControlRegression',ControlRequest,run_id=ID),'regressions:run',True),
    'save_draft': Tool('保存个人命名空间内的独立工作流草稿，不改共享工作副本。',args('SaveDraft',DraftRequest,draft_key=KEY),'workflows:write',True),
    'get_draft': Tool('读取本人命名空间中的最新草稿。',args('GetDraft',draft_key=KEY),'workflows:read'),
    'freeze_draft': Tool('校验并冻结个人草稿成为新机制版本，不激活生产。',args('FreezeDraft',FreezeRequest,draft_key=KEY),'workflows:write',True),
    'create_component': Tool('复制为独立组件，不修改源组件或线上共享配置。',ComponentRequest,'evaluation_components:write',True),
    'get_component': Tool('读取组件工作副本。',args('GetComponent',asset_id=ID),'workflows:read'),
}


def schema(tool):
    return tool.model.model_json_schema()


def require_row(db, model, ident, *, lock=True):
    query=select(model).where(model.id==ident)
    row = db.scalar((query.with_for_update() if lock else query).execution_options(populate_existing=True))
    if row is None:
        raise HTTPException(404, '对象不存在')
    return row


def categories(db, name, a):
    from .models import Asset,BaselineSet,BaselineSetItem,BaselineRegressionRun,WorkflowNodeAsset
    from .employee_access import object_categories
    result=set()
    if 'source_version_id' in a or 'workflow_version_id' in a:
        vid=a.get('source_version_id',a.get('workflow_version_id'))
        _,manifest=lab._require_version(db,vid)
        result.add(manifest.get('category_key'))
    for key in ('baseline_set_id','source_set_id','set_id'):
        if key in a:
            sid=a[key]
            result.add(require_row(db,BaselineSet,sid).category_key)
            query=select(BaselineSetItem).where(BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id==sid)
            if name=='confirm_reference': query=query.where(BaselineSetItem.id==a['item_id'])
            if name=='clone_samples': query=query.where(BaselineSetItem.id.in_(a['item_ids']))
            for item in db.scalars(query.order_by(BaselineSetItem.id).with_for_update()):
                result.add(require_row(db,Asset,item.asset_id).category_key)
    if 'run_id' in a:
        result |= object_categories(db,'baseline_run',{'run_id':a['run_id']},{})
    if name=='create_component': result.add(require_row(db,WorkflowNodeAsset,a['source_asset_id']).category_key)
    if name=='import_samples':
        result.add(a['category_key'])
        for item in a['items']: result.add(require_row(db,Asset,item['asset_id']).category_key)
    if name=='freeze_draft':
        draft=stock.read_draft(db,a['draft_key'])
        _,manifest=lab._require_version(db,draft['source_version_id'])
        result.add(manifest.get('category_key'))
    return result


def require_owned_run(db,user,run_id):
    from .models import AuditEvent,BaselineRegressionRun
    run=require_row(db,BaselineRegressionRun,run_id)
    owner=db.scalar(select(AuditEvent.id).where(AuditEvent.category=='personal_mcp',
        AuditEvent.action=='start_regression',AuditEvent.actor==f'mcp:{user.id}',
        AuditEvent.subject_type=='baseline_regression_run',AuditEvent.subject_id==str(run_id)).limit(1))
    if run.created_by!=lab.LAB_ACTOR or owner is None:
        raise HTTPException(403,'MCP只能控制本人通过MCP发起的存量回归')


def reserve_retry_quota(db,run_id,a):
    """Called under the shared LabWriteLock, before any retry mutates state."""
    from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
    from .models import AuditEvent,BaselineRegressionRun
    key=f'mcp-retry:{run_id}:{a["request_id"]}'
    if stock.event(db,key): return
    start=datetime.now(timezone(timedelta(hours=8))).replace(hour=0,minute=0,second=0,microsecond=0).astimezone(timezone.utc).replace(tzinfo=None)
    used=int(db.scalar(select(func.coalesce(func.sum(BaselineRegressionRun.total),0)).where(
        BaselineRegressionRun.created_by==lab.LAB_ACTOR,BaselineRegressionRun.created_at>=start)) or 0)
    for payload in db.scalars(select(AuditEvent.payload_json).where(AuditEvent.category=='personal_mcp_retry',AuditEvent.created_at>=start)):
        used+=json.loads(payload)['count']
    count=len(set(a['item_ids']))
    if used+count>lab.daily_item_cap():
        raise lab.PromptLabError('lab_daily_cap_reached','本次补跑会超过实验室每日共享额度',status_code=429)
    other=db.scalar(select(BaselineRegressionRun.id).where(BaselineRegressionRun.created_by==lab.LAB_ACTOR,
        BaselineRegressionRun.status=='running',BaselineRegressionRun.id!=run_id).limit(1))
    if other is not None:
        raise lab.PromptLabError('lab_run_in_progress','另一轮实验室回归仍在运行，暂不补跑',status_code=409)
    from .audit import append_audit_event
    append_audit_event(db,category='personal_mcp_retry',action='retry_reserved',subject_type='baseline_regression_run',
        subject_id=run_id,actor='mcp-service',event_key=key,payload={'count':count})


def cancel_owned_run(db,run_id):
    from .models import BaselineRegressionRun,BaselineRegressionItem
    run=require_row(db,BaselineRegressionRun,run_id)
    ids=list(db.scalars(select(BaselineRegressionItem.id).where(
        BaselineRegressionItem.run_id==run_id,BaselineRegressionItem.status.in_(['queued','running']))
        .order_by(BaselineRegressionItem.id)))
    if not ids: return {**lab.run_summary(run),'cancelled_items':0}
    # The stock service locks all selected children and rejects the whole request
    # if a provider call is still in flight. Completed facts remain untouched.
    receipt=stock.control_items(db,run_id=run_id,item_ids=ids,action='cancel',request_id=f'mcp-cancel-{run_id}')
    db.flush();db.refresh(run)
    return {**lab.run_summary(run),'cancelled_items':len(receipt['items'])}


def invoke(db,user,name,arguments):
    tool=TOOLS.get(name)
    if tool is None: raise HTTPException(404,'未知MCP工具')
    if not has_permission(user,tool.permission): raise HTTPException(403,'当前账号没有此操作权限')
    a=tool.model.model_validate(arguments,strict=True).model_dump()
    if 'draft_key' in a:
        check_key(a['draft_key'])
        a['draft_key']=personal_key(user.id,a['draft_key'])
    if 'request_id' in a: a['request_id']=personal_key(user.id,a['request_id'])
    if a.get('client_request_id'): a['client_request_id']=personal_key(user.id,a['client_request_id'])
    if tool.write:
        require_categories(user,categories(db,name,a))
    if name in {'control_regression_items','cancel_regression'}:
        require_owned_run(db,user,a['run_id'])
    if name=='platform_capabilities':
        return {'user_id':user.id,'tools':[n for n,t in TOOLS.items() if has_permission(user,t.permission)],'production_write':False,'production_activation':False,'incremental_switch_write':False,'shared_config_write':False,'run_control':'own_mcp_runs_only','shared_lab_limits':{'max_active_runs':lab.MAX_ACTIVE_RUNS,'daily_items':lab.daily_item_cap()}}
    if name=='stock_guide': return {'guide':GUIDE,'authentication':'个人MCP令牌；不使用GUIDE中的共享机器令牌'}
    if name=='catalog':
        from .models import ModelRegistryEntry,PromptVersion,RuleModuleVersion,EvaluationCategoryProfile,WorkflowNodeAsset
        return {
            'categories':[{'key':r.category_key,'status':r.status} for r in db.scalars(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile))],
            'models':[{'id':r.id,'name':r.name,'model_id':r.model_id} for r in db.scalars(select(ModelRegistryEntry))],
            'prompts':[{'id':r.id,'category_key':r.category_key,'version':r.version} for r in db.scalars(select(PromptVersion).order_by(PromptVersion.id.desc()).limit(500))],
            'rules':[{'id':r.id,'module_type_key':r.module_type_key,'version':r.version} for r in db.scalars(select(RuleModuleVersion).order_by(RuleModuleVersion.id.desc()).limit(500))],
            'components':[{'id':r.id,'asset_key':r.asset_key,'category_key':r.category_key,'kind':r.kind} for r in db.scalars(select(WorkflowNodeAsset).order_by(WorkflowNodeAsset.id.desc()).limit(500))],
            'component_limit':500,
        }
    if name=='read_asset_image':
        import base64
        from .config import get_settings
        from .nas_storage import resolve_asset_path, NasStorageError
        asset=lab.require_asset(db,a['asset_id'])
        if asset.mime_type not in {'image/png','image/jpeg','image/webp','image/gif'}:
            raise HTTPException(422,'此工具仅支持PNG、JPEG、WebP、GIF图片')
        try:
            path=resolve_asset_path(asset,get_settings())
            with path.open('rb') as stream: data=stream.read(10*1024*1024+1)
        except (NasStorageError,OSError):
            raise HTTPException(404,'图片文件缺失或不可用') from None
        if len(data)>10*1024*1024: raise HTTPException(413,'图片超过10MiB，请在中台查看原文件')
        return {'mimeType':asset.mime_type,'data':base64.b64encode(data).decode()}
    if name=='list_mechanisms': return lab.list_mechanisms_page(db,**a)
    if name=='get_mechanism': return lab.describe_mechanism(db,a['version_id'])
    if name=='get_manifest': return stock.manifest_payload(db,a['version_id'])
    if name=='create_candidate':
        a['edits']={k:lab.PromptEdit(**v) for k,v in a.pop('prompts').items()}
        return lab.create_candidate(db,**a)
    if name=='list_sample_sets': return {'items':lab.list_baseline_sets(db)}
    if name=='get_reference': return stock.truth_payload(db,**a,lock=False)
    if name=='confirm_reference': return stock.write_truth(db,**a)
    if name=='clone_samples': return stock.clone_baseline(db,**a)
    if name=='import_samples': return stock.import_baseline(db,**a)
    if name=='start_regression': return lab.start_run(db,**a)
    if name=='list_regressions': return {'items':lab.list_runs(db,**a)}
    if name=='get_regression': return lab.get_run(db,a['run_id'])
    if name=='regression_items': return lab.run_items(db,**a)
    if name=='cancel_regression': return cancel_owned_run(db,a['run_id'])
    if name=='control_regression_items':
        if a['action']=='retry': reserve_retry_quota(db,a['run_id'],a)
        return stock.control_items(db,**a)
    if name=='save_draft': return stock.save_draft(db,**a)
    if name=='get_draft': return stock.read_draft(db,**a)
    if name=='freeze_draft': return stock.freeze_draft(db,**a)
    if name=='create_component': return stock.create_component(db,**a)
    if name=='get_component':
        from .evaluation_components import require_component_asset,get_component_working_copy
        row=require_component_asset(db,a['asset_id']); working=get_component_working_copy(db,row.id)
        return {'asset_id':row.id,'asset_key':row.asset_key,'category_key':row.category_key,'kind':row.kind,'content':json.loads(working.content_json),'revision':working.lock_version}
    if name=='list_sample_items':
        from .models import BaselineSet,BaselineSetItem
        require_row(db,BaselineSet,a['set_id'],lock=False)
        ids=db.scalars(select(BaselineSetItem.id).where(BaselineSetItem.baseline_set_id==a['set_id']).order_by(BaselineSetItem.id).offset(a['offset']).limit(a['limit']+1)).all()
        return {'items':[stock.truth_payload(db,a['set_id'],i,lock=False) for i in ids[:a['limit']]],'next_offset':a['offset']+a['limit'] if len(ids)>a['limit'] else None}
    raise HTTPException(404,'工具未实现')

===== FILE backend/app/postgres_importer.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import Engine, Table, func, inspect, select, text
from sqlalchemy.dialects.postgresql import insert as postgresql_insert
from sqlalchemy.exc import SQLAlchemyError

from . import models as _models  # noqa: F401
from .database import Base
from .snapshot_import_common import (
    ImportPlan,
    ImportReport,
    InjectedImportStop,
    SnapshotImportError,
    load_checkpoint,
    open_immutable_sqlite,
    resolve_source_snapshot,
    source_batch,
    source_schema_compatibility_version,
    validate_source_schema,
    verify_source_unchanged,
    write_checkpoint,
)


PostgresImportError = SnapshotImportError
_validate_source_schema = validate_source_schema


def _assert_target_ready(
    target: Engine,
    *,
    checkpoint: dict[str, Any],
    resumed: bool,
) -> None:
    if target.dialect.name != "postgresql":
        raise PostgresImportError("导入目标必须是 PostgreSQL")
    actual_tables = set(inspect(target).get_table_names())
    missing = set(Base.metadata.tables) - actual_tables
    if missing:
        raise PostgresImportError(
            f"PostgreSQL 目标缺少基线表：{sorted(missing)}"
        )
    progress = checkpoint["tables"]
    with target.connect() as connection:
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
            actual_count = int(
                connection.scalar(select(func.count()).select_from(table)) or 0
            )
            if not resumed and actual_count:
                raise PostgresImportError(
                    f"PostgreSQL 目标表 {table.name} 非空，禁止首次合并导入"
                )
            if resumed and table.name in progress:
                recorded_count = int(progress[table.name]["imported_rows"])
                if actual_count < recorded_count:
                    raise PostgresImportError(
                        f"PostgreSQL 目标表 {table.name} 少于检查点记录"
                    )


def _insert_batch(target: Engine, table: Table, rows: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> int:
    primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
    statement = postgresql_insert(table).values(rows).on_conflict_do_nothing(
        index_elements=[primary_key.name]
    ).returning(primary_key)
    try:
        with target.begin() as connection:
            connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SET LOCAL session_replication_role = replica"
            )
            result = connection.execute(statement)
            inserted_primary_keys = result.scalars().all()
    except SQLAlchemyError:
        raise PostgresImportError(
            f"PostgreSQL 导入表 {table.name} 的批次失败；行内容已隐藏"
        ) from None
    return len(inserted_primary_keys)


def _reset_postgres_sequences(target: Engine) -> None:
    with target.begin() as connection:
        for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
            primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
            sequence_name = connection.scalar(
                text("SELECT pg_get_serial_sequence(:table_name, :column_name)"),
                {
                    "table_name": table.name,
                    "column_name": primary_key.name,
                },
            )
            if sequence_name is None:
                continue
            maximum = connection.scalar(select(func.max(primary_key)))
            connection.execute(
                text(
                    "SELECT setval(CAST(:sequence_name AS regclass), "
                    ":value, :is_called)"
                ),
                {
                    "sequence_name": sequence_name,
                    "value": 1 if maximum is None else int(maximum),
                    "is_called": maximum is not None,
                },
            )


def import_sqlite_snapshot(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1000,
    stop_after_batches: int | None = None,
) -> ImportReport:
    """Import a frozen SQLite snapshot in committed, resumable PK batches."""
    if batch_size <= 0:
        raise PostgresImportError("batch_size 必须大于 0")
    if stop_after_batches is not None and stop_after_batches <= 0:
        raise PostgresImportError("stop_after_batches 必须大于 0")
    resolved_source, source_stat, source_sha256 = resolve_source_snapshot(source)
    source_connection = open_immutable_sqlite(resolved_source)
    inserted_rows = 0
    duplicate_rows = 0
    committed_batches = 0
    try:
        source_columns_by_table = validate_source_schema(
            source_connection,
            Base.metadata,
        )
        source_schema_version = source_schema_compatibility_version(
            source_connection
        )
        complete_plan = ImportPlan.from_metadata(Base.metadata)
        plan = ImportPlan(
            tuple(
                table_name
                for table_name in complete_plan
                if table_name in source_columns_by_table
            )
        )
        checkpoint_path = checkpoint_dir / "sqlite-to-postgres-import.json"
        checkpoint, resumed = load_checkpoint(
            checkpoint_path,
            source=resolved_source,
            source_size=source_stat.st_size,
            source_sha256=source_sha256,
            plan=plan,
            format_name="sqlite-to-postgres-import-v1",
        )
        _assert_target_ready(target, checkpoint=checkpoint, resumed=resumed)
        table_progress: dict[str, Any] = checkpoint["tables"]
        for table_name in plan:
            table = Base.metadata.tables[table_name]
            progress = table_progress.setdefault(
                table_name,
                {
                    "completed": False,
                    "imported_rows": 0,
                    "last_primary_key": None,
                },
            )
            if progress["completed"]:
                continue
            while True:
                rows = source_batch(
                    source_connection,
                    table,
                    last_primary_key=progress["last_primary_key"],
                    batch_size=batch_size,
                    source_columns=source_columns_by_table[table_name],
                    source_schema_version=source_schema_version,
                )
                if not rows:
                    progress["completed"] = True
                    write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
                    break
                inserted = _insert_batch(target, table, rows)
                inserted_rows += inserted
                duplicate_rows += len(rows) - inserted
                primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
                progress["last_primary_key"] = rows[-1][primary_key.name]
                progress["imported_rows"] = int(
                    progress["imported_rows"]
                ) + len(rows)
                write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
                committed_batches += 1
                if (
                    stop_after_batches is not None
                    and committed_batches >= stop_after_batches
                ):
                    raise InjectedImportStop(
                        f"在第 {committed_batches} 个已提交批次后注入停止"
                    )
        _reset_postgres_sequences(target)
        checkpoint["complete"] = True
        write_checkpoint(checkpoint_path, checkpoint)
        table_rows = {
            table_name: int(progress["imported_rows"])
            for table_name, progress in checkpoint["tables"].items()
        }
        return ImportReport(
            source=resolved_source,
            source_sha256=source_sha256,
            inserted_rows=inserted_rows,
            duplicate_rows=duplicate_rows,
            table_rows=table_rows,
            completed_tables=sum(
                1
                for progress in checkpoint["tables"].values()
                if progress["completed"]
            ),
            resumed=resumed,
        )
    finally:
        source_connection.close()
        verify_source_unchanged(
            resolved_source,
            initial_stat=source_stat,
            initial_sha256=source_sha256,
        )


def resume_import(
    source: Path,
    target: Engine,
    checkpoint_dir: Path,
    *,
    batch_size: int = 1000,
) -> ImportReport:
    return import_sqlite_snapshot(
        source,
        target,
        checkpoint_dir,
        batch_size=batch_size,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/postgres_integration.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import getpass
import hashlib
import json
import os
import plistlib
import re
import shlex
import shutil
import sys
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from subprocess import DEVNULL, PIPE, CompletedProcess, Popen, run
from typing import Any, Callable, Sequence, TextIO


INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT = Path("/Volumes/WorkSSD")
INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME = "3d66-label-system-integration"
PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES = 120 * 1024**3
MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES = 40 * 1024**3


class IntegrationPathError(ValueError):
    """Raised when an integration path could escape the approved SSD directory."""


class IntegrationCapacityError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the integration workspace reaches a storage safety limit."""


class PostgresIntegrationError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when the isolated PostgreSQL cluster cannot be managed safely."""


class SnapshotVerificationError(PostgresIntegrationError):
    """Raised when a migration snapshot is missing, mutable, or has the wrong digest."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SnapshotManifest:
    path: Path
    size_bytes: int
    sha256: str
    source: str

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, int | str]:
        return {
            "path": str(self.path),
            "size_bytes": self.size_bytes,
            "sha256": self.sha256,
            "source": self.source,
        }


def load_snapshot_manifest(snapshot: Path) -> SnapshotManifest:
    manifest_path = snapshot.with_name(f"{snapshot.name}.manifest.json")
    if manifest_path.is_symlink() or not manifest_path.is_file():
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单不存在或不是普通文件")
    try:
        payload = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单 JSON 无法读取") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单 JSON 必须是对象")
    try:
        manifest = SnapshotManifest(
            path=Path(str(payload["path"])),
            size_bytes=int(payload["size_bytes"]),
            sha256=str(payload["sha256"]),
            source=str(payload["source"]),
        )
    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单字段不完整") from exc
    if manifest.path != snapshot:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照清单路径与导入文件不一致")
    verify_snapshot(manifest)
    return manifest


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PostgresBinaries:
    initdb: Path
    pg_ctl: Path
    postgres: Path
    psql: Path
    createdb: Path

    @classmethod
    def for_prefix(cls, prefix: Path) -> "PostgresBinaries":
        binary_dir = prefix.expanduser().resolve(strict=False) / "bin"
        return cls(
            initdb=binary_dir / "initdb",
            pg_ctl=binary_dir / "pg_ctl",
            postgres=binary_dir / "postgres",
            psql=binary_dir / "psql",
            createdb=binary_dir / "createdb",
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class IntegrationPaths:
    root: Path
    pgdata: Path
    socket_dir: Path
    logs: Path
    snapshots: Path
    checkpoints: Path
    reports: Path

    @classmethod
    def from_root(cls, root: Path) -> "IntegrationPaths":
        resolved_root = root.expanduser().resolve(strict=False)
        return cls(
            root=resolved_root,
            pgdata=resolved_root / "pgdata",
            socket_dir=resolved_root / "socket",
            logs=resolved_root / "logs",
            snapshots=resolved_root / "snapshots",
            checkpoints=resolved_root / "checkpoints",
            reports=resolved_root / "reports",
        )

    def assert_safe(self) -> None:
        approved_parent = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.resolve(strict=False)
        if (
            self.root.name != INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME
            or self.root.parent != approved_parent
        ):
            raise IntegrationPathError(
                "PostgreSQL 集成数据只能位于 WorkSSD 的项目专属目录"
            )
        for child in (
            self.pgdata,
            self.socket_dir,
            self.logs,
            self.snapshots,
            self.checkpoints,
            self.reports,
        ):
            if child.is_symlink():
                raise IntegrationPathError(
                    f"项目专属目录内不得使用符号链接：{child.name}"
                )
            if child.exists() and not child.resolve(strict=True).is_relative_to(
                self.root
            ):
                raise IntegrationPathError(
                    f"项目专属目录子路径越界：{child.name}"
                )


def _directory_size(root: Path) -> int:
    if not root.exists():
        return 0
    total = 0
    pending = [root]
    while pending:
        directory = pending.pop()
        with os.scandir(directory) as entries:
            for entry in entries:
                if entry.is_symlink():
                    raise IntegrationPathError(
                        f"项目专属目录内不得使用符号链接：{entry.name}"
                    )
                if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                    pending.append(Path(entry.path))
                elif entry.is_file(follow_symlinks=False):
                    total += entry.stat(follow_symlinks=False).st_size
    return total


def integration_disk_report(paths: IntegrationPaths) -> dict[str, int | str]:
    paths.assert_safe()
    project_used_bytes = _directory_size(paths.root)
    disk = shutil.disk_usage(paths.root if paths.root.exists() else paths.root.parent)
    if project_used_bytes >= PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError(
            "项目集成目录已达到 120 GiB 安全上限，停止写入"
        )
    if disk.free < MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError(
            "WorkSSD 可用空间低于 40 GiB 安全下限，停止写入"
        )
    return {
        "root": str(paths.root),
        "project_used_bytes": project_used_bytes,
        "project_limit_bytes": PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES,
        "filesystem_total_bytes": disk.total,
        "filesystem_free_bytes": disk.free,
        "minimum_free_bytes": MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES,
    }


CommandRunner = Callable[[Sequence[str]], CompletedProcess[str] | object]


def _default_run_command(command: Sequence[str]) -> CompletedProcess[str]:
    return run(
        list(command),
        check=False,
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        encoding="utf-8",
        errors="replace",
    )


def _run_checked(command: Sequence[str], runner: CommandRunner) -> None:
    completed = runner(command)
    returncode = getattr(completed, "returncode", 0)
    if returncode != 0:
        stderr = str(getattr(completed, "stderr", "")).strip()
        stdout = str(getattr(completed, "stdout", "")).strip()
        detail = stderr or stdout or f"exit {returncode}"
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"PostgreSQL 命令失败（{Path(command[0]).name}）：{detail}"
        )


def _atomic_write_text(path: Path, content: str, *, mode: int) -> None:
    path.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    descriptor, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
        prefix=f".{path.name}.",
        dir=path.parent,
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "w", encoding="utf-8") as output:
            output.write(content)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
        os.chmod(temporary_name, mode)
        os.replace(temporary_name, path)
    finally:
        if os.path.exists(temporary_name):
            os.unlink(temporary_name)


def _fsync_directory(path: Path) -> None:
    descriptor = os.open(path, os.O_RDONLY)
    try:
        os.fsync(descriptor)
    finally:
        os.close(descriptor)


def _normalized_sha256(value: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip().lower()
    if re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", normalized) is None:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("期望 SHA-256 必须是 64 位十六进制")
    return normalized


def _file_sha256(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as source:
        while chunk := source.read(1024 * 1024):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def verify_snapshot(manifest: SnapshotManifest) -> None:
    """Verify the immutable local snapshot against its frozen manifest."""
    path = manifest.path
    if path.is_symlink() or not path.is_file():
        raise SnapshotVerificationError(f"快照文件不存在或不是普通文件：{path}")
    expected_sha256 = _normalized_sha256(manifest.sha256)
    actual_size = path.stat().st_size
    actual_sha256 = _file_sha256(path)
    actual_mode = path.stat().st_mode & 0o777
    failures: list[str] = []
    if actual_size != manifest.size_bytes:
        failures.append(
            f"大小期望 {manifest.size_bytes}，实际 {actual_size}"
        )
    if actual_sha256 != expected_sha256:
        failures.append(
            f"SHA-256 期望 {expected_sha256}，实际 {actual_sha256}"
        )
    if actual_mode != 0o400:
        failures.append(f"文件必须为 0400 只读，实际 {actual_mode:04o}")
    if failures:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照校验失败：" + "；".join(failures))


def _write_snapshot_manifest(manifest: SnapshotManifest) -> Path:
    manifest_path = manifest.path.with_name(f"{manifest.path.name}.manifest.json")
    _atomic_write_text(
        manifest_path,
        json.dumps(
            manifest.as_dict(),
            ensure_ascii=False,
            indent=2,
            sort_keys=True,
        )
        + "\n",
        mode=0o400,
    )
    _fsync_directory(manifest_path.parent)
    return manifest_path


def promote_verified_snapshot(
    partial: Path,
    final: Path,
    *,
    expected_sha256: str,
    expected_size: int,
    source: str,
) -> SnapshotManifest:
    """Atomically promote one complete verified partial without overwriting facts."""
    expected_digest = _normalized_sha256(expected_sha256)
    if expected_size < 0:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("期望快照大小不能为负数")
    manifest = SnapshotManifest(
        path=final,
        size_bytes=expected_size,
        sha256=expected_digest,
        source=source.strip(),
    )
    if not manifest.source:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照来源不能为空")
    if final.exists() or final.is_symlink():
        verify_snapshot(manifest)
        _write_snapshot_manifest(manifest)
        return manifest
    if partial.is_symlink() or not partial.is_file():
        raise SnapshotVerificationError(
            f"待校验快照不存在或不是普通文件：{partial}"
        )
    actual_size = partial.stat().st_size
    actual_sha256 = _file_sha256(partial)
    failures: list[str] = []
    if actual_size != expected_size:
        failures.append(f"大小期望 {expected_size}，实际 {actual_size}")
    if actual_sha256 != expected_digest:
        failures.append(
            f"SHA-256 期望 {expected_digest}，实际 {actual_sha256}"
        )
    if failures:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照校验失败：" + "；".join(failures))
    with partial.open("rb") as source_file:
        os.fsync(source_file.fileno())
    os.chmod(partial, 0o400)
    os.replace(partial, final)
    _fsync_directory(final.parent)
    verify_snapshot(manifest)
    _write_snapshot_manifest(manifest)
    return manifest


PopenFactory = Callable[..., Any]


def copy_snapshot_from_ssh(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    *,
    remote_host: str,
    remote_path: Path,
    expected_sha256: str,
    expected_size: int,
    ssh_binary: Path = Path("/usr/bin/ssh"),
    identity_file: Path | None = None,
    sudo_read: bool = False,
    popen_factory: PopenFactory = Popen,
) -> SnapshotManifest:
    """Stream an approved remote SQLite snapshot into the isolated workspace."""
    paths.assert_safe()
    host = remote_host.strip()
    if re.fullmatch(r"(?:[A-Za-z0-9._-]+@)?[A-Za-z0-9._:-]+", host) is None:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("远程主机格式不安全")
    if not remote_path.is_absolute() or any(
        character in str(remote_path) for character in ("\n", "\r", "\0")
    ):
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("远程快照必须是安全的绝对路径")
    if expected_size < 0:
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("期望快照大小不能为负数")
    expected_digest = _normalized_sha256(expected_sha256)
    report = integration_disk_report(paths)
    if int(report["project_used_bytes"]) + expected_size >= PROJECT_LIMIT_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError("复制快照会超过项目 120 GiB 安全上限")
    if int(report["filesystem_free_bytes"]) - expected_size < MINIMUM_FREE_BYTES:
        raise IntegrationCapacityError("复制快照会使 WorkSSD 低于 40 GiB 安全下限")
    _prepare_directories(paths)
    final = paths.snapshots / remote_path.name
    partial = final.with_name(f"{final.name}.partial")
    source = f"{host}:{remote_path}"
    manifest = SnapshotManifest(
        path=final,
        size_bytes=expected_size,
        sha256=expected_digest,
        source=source,
    )
    if final.exists() or final.is_symlink():
        verify_snapshot(manifest)
        _write_snapshot_manifest(manifest)
        return manifest
    if partial.is_symlink():
        raise SnapshotVerificationError("快照临时文件不得为符号链接")
    identity_arguments: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    if identity_file is not None:
        expanded_identity = identity_file.expanduser()
        if expanded_identity.is_symlink() or not expanded_identity.is_file():
            raise SnapshotVerificationError("SSH 身份文件不存在或不是普通文件")
        resolved_identity = expanded_identity.resolve(strict=True)
        if resolved_identity.stat().st_mode & 0o077:
            raise SnapshotVerificationError(
                "SSH 身份文件权限不得向组或其他用户开放"
            )
        identity_arguments = ("-i", str(resolved_identity))
    command = (
        str(ssh_binary),
        *identity_arguments,
        "-o",
        "BatchMode=yes",
        "--",
        host,
        ("sudo -n " if sudo_read else "")
        + f"cat -- {shlex.quote(str(remote_path))}",
    )
    process = popen_factory(command, stdout=PIPE, stderr=DEVNULL)
    stdout = getattr(process, "stdout", None)
    if stdout is None:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("SSH 快照流缺少标准输出")
    descriptor = os.open(
        partial,
        os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0),
        0o600,
    )
    try:
        with os.fdopen(descriptor, "wb") as output:
            while chunk := stdout.read(1024 * 1024):
                output.write(chunk)
            output.flush()
            os.fsync(output.fileno())
    finally:
        stdout.close()
    returncode = int(process.wait())
    stderr = getattr(process, "stderr", None)
    if stderr is not None:
        stderr.close()
    if returncode != 0:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"SSH 快照复制失败（exit {returncode}），未晋升临时文件"
        )
    return promote_verified_snapshot(
        partial,
        final,
        expected_sha256=expected_digest,
        expected_size=expected_size,
        source=source,
    )


def _postgres_literal(value: Path) -> str:
    rendered = str(value)
    if "\n" in rendered or "\r" in rendered:
        raise IntegrationPathError("PostgreSQL 路径不能包含换行符")
    return rendered.replace("'", "''")


def _prepare_directories(paths: IntegrationPaths) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    approved_parent = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.resolve(strict=False)
    if not approved_parent.is_dir():
        raise IntegrationPathError("WorkSSD 未挂载，禁止创建集成目录")
    paths.root.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
    os.chmod(paths.root, 0o700)
    for directory in (
        paths.socket_dir,
        paths.logs,
        paths.snapshots,
        paths.checkpoints,
        paths.reports,
    ):
        directory.mkdir(mode=0o700, exist_ok=True)
        os.chmod(directory, 0o700)
    paths.assert_safe()


def _postgres_auto_configuration(paths: IntegrationPaths) -> str:
    return "\n".join(
        (
            "# Managed by 3d66 label-system local integration tooling.",
            "listen_addresses = ''",
            f"unix_socket_directories = '{_postgres_literal(paths.socket_dir)}'",
            "unix_socket_permissions = 0700",
            "ssl = off",
            "logging_collector = on",
            f"log_directory = '{_postgres_literal(paths.logs)}'",
            "log_filename = 'postgresql-%Y-%m-%d.log'",
            "",
        )
    )


def initialize_postgres(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> None:
    integration_disk_report(paths)
    _prepare_directories(paths)
    version_file = paths.pgdata / "PG_VERSION"
    if version_file.exists():
        major = version_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        if major != "16":
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                f"已有 PostgreSQL 数据目录版本为 {major or '未知'}，要求版本 16"
            )
    else:
        if paths.pgdata.exists() and any(paths.pgdata.iterdir()):
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                "已有 pgdata 目录非空但缺少 PG_VERSION，禁止覆盖"
            )
        _run_checked(
            (
                str(binaries.initdb),
                "--pgdata",
                str(paths.pgdata),
                "--encoding=UTF8",
                "--locale=C",
                "--auth-local=trust",
                "--auth-host=reject",
            ),
            run_command,
        )
        if not version_file.is_file():
            raise PostgresIntegrationError("initdb 完成后未生成 PG_VERSION")
        major = version_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
        if major != "16":
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                f"initdb 生成版本为 {major or '未知'}，要求版本 16"
            )
    _atomic_write_text(
        paths.pgdata / "postgresql.auto.conf",
        _postgres_auto_configuration(paths),
        mode=0o600,
    )


def _default_filesystem_type(path: Path) -> str:
    completed = _default_run_command(
        ("/usr/sbin/diskutil", "info", "-plist", str(path))
    )
    if completed.returncode != 0:
        detail = completed.stderr.strip() or completed.stdout.strip()
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"无法识别 WorkSSD 文件系统：{detail or completed.returncode}"
        )
    try:
        payload = plistlib.loads(completed.stdout.encode("utf-8"))
    except (plistlib.InvalidFileException, ValueError) as exc:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("无法解析 WorkSSD 文件系统信息") from exc
    if not isinstance(payload, dict):
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("WorkSSD 文件系统信息不是属性字典")
    filesystem = payload.get("FilesystemType") or payload.get("FilesystemName")
    if not isinstance(filesystem, str) or not filesystem.strip():
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("WorkSSD 文件系统信息缺少类型")
    return filesystem.strip().lower()


def _require_binaries(binaries: PostgresBinaries) -> None:
    missing = [
        str(path)
        for path in (
            binaries.initdb,
            binaries.pg_ctl,
            binaries.postgres,
            binaries.psql,
            binaries.createdb,
        )
        if not path.is_file() or not os.access(path, os.X_OK)
    ]
    if missing:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            "PostgreSQL 16 可执行文件不完整：" + "、".join(missing)
        )


def preflight_postgres(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    filesystem_type: Callable[[Path], str] = _default_filesystem_type,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
    require_executables: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, int | str | bool]:
    report = integration_disk_report(paths)
    filesystem_probe_path = INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT.resolve(strict=True)
    filesystem = filesystem_type(filesystem_probe_path).strip().lower()
    if filesystem != "apfs":
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"集成数据盘必须使用 APFS，当前识别为 {filesystem or '未知'}"
        )
    if require_executables:
        _require_binaries(binaries)
    completed = run_command((str(binaries.postgres), "--version"))
    returncode = getattr(completed, "returncode", 0)
    if returncode != 0:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("无法读取 PostgreSQL 版本")
    version_text = str(getattr(completed, "stdout", "")).strip()
    match = re.search(r"PostgreSQL\)\s+(\d+)(?:\.\d+)?", version_text)
    if match is None:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("无法解析 PostgreSQL 版本")
    major = int(match.group(1))
    if major != 16:
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"本地集成要求 PostgreSQL 16，当前为 {major}"
        )
    return {
        **report,
        "filesystem": filesystem,
        "postgres_major": major,
        "postgres_version": version_text,
        "socket_only": True,
    }


def _require_cluster_version(paths: IntegrationPaths) -> None:
    paths.assert_safe()
    version_file = paths.pgdata / "PG_VERSION"
    if not version_file.is_file():
        raise PostgresIntegrationError("PostgreSQL 集成集群尚未初始化")
    major = version_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    if major != "16":
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"已有 PostgreSQL 数据目录版本为 {major or '未知'}，要求版本 16"
        )


def start_postgres(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> None:
    integration_disk_report(paths)
    _prepare_directories(paths)
    _require_cluster_version(paths)
    _run_checked(
        (
            str(binaries.pg_ctl),
            "--pgdata",
            str(paths.pgdata),
            "--log",
            str(paths.logs / "postgres-startup.log"),
            "--wait",
            "start",
        ),
        run_command,
    )


def stop_postgres(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> None:
    _require_cluster_version(paths)
    _run_checked(
        (
            str(binaries.pg_ctl),
            "--pgdata",
            str(paths.pgdata),
            "--mode=fast",
            "--wait",
            "stop",
        ),
        run_command,
    )


def postgres_status(
    paths: IntegrationPaths,
    binaries: PostgresBinaries,
    *,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
) -> dict[str, bool | str]:
    paths.assert_safe()
    completed = run_command(
        (
            str(binaries.pg_ctl),
            "--pgdata",
            str(paths.pgdata),
            "status",
        )
    )
    returncode = int(getattr(completed, "returncode", 1))
    if returncode not in {0, 3}:
        stderr = str(getattr(completed, "stderr", "")).strip()
        stdout = str(getattr(completed, "stdout", "")).strip()
        raise PostgresIntegrationError(
            f"无法读取 PostgreSQL 状态：{stderr or stdout or returncode}"
        )
    return {
        "running": returncode == 0,
        "pgdata": str(paths.pgdata),
        "socket_dir": str(paths.socket_dir),
    }


def _cli_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        description="Manage the isolated 3d66 label-system PostgreSQL 16 cluster."
    )
    commands = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    for command_name in ("preflight", "init", "start", "stop", "status"):
        command = commands.add_parser(command_name)
        command.add_argument(
            "--root",
            type=Path,
            default=INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME,
        )
        command.add_argument(
            "--postgres-prefix",
            type=Path,
            default=Path("/opt/homebrew/opt/postgresql@16"),
        )
    snapshot = commands.add_parser("copy-snapshot")
    snapshot.add_argument(
        "--root",
        type=Path,
        default=INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME,
    )
    snapshot.add_argument("--remote-host", required=True)
    snapshot.add_argument("--remote-path", type=Path, required=True)
    snapshot.add_argument("--expected-sha256", required=True)
    snapshot.add_argument("--expected-size", type=int, required=True)
    snapshot.add_argument("--identity-file", type=Path)
    snapshot.add_argument("--sudo-read", action="store_true")
    importer = commands.add_parser("import")
    importer.add_argument(
        "--root",
        type=Path,
        default=INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME,
    )
    importer.add_argument("--source", type=Path, required=True)
    importer.add_argument("--database", required=True)
    importer.add_argument("--database-user", default=getpass.getuser())
    importer.add_argument("--batch-size", type=int, default=1000)
    importer.add_argument("--checkpoint-dir", type=Path)
    reconciler = commands.add_parser("reconcile")
    reconciler.add_argument(
        "--root",
        type=Path,
        default=INTEGRATION_VOLUME_ROOT / INTEGRATION_DIRECTORY_NAME,
    )
    reconciler.add_argument("--source", type=Path, required=True)
    reconciler.add_argument("--database", required=True)
    reconciler.add_argument("--database-user", default=getpass.getuser())
    reconciler.add_argument("--report-name")
    return parser


def cli_main(
    argv: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    *,
    filesystem_type: Callable[[Path], str] = _default_filesystem_type,
    run_command: CommandRunner = _default_run_command,
    popen_factory: PopenFactory = Popen,
    output: TextIO = sys.stdout,
) -> int:
    args = _cli_parser().parse_args(argv)
    paths = IntegrationPaths.from_root(args.root)

    if args.command == "copy-snapshot":
        manifest = copy_snapshot_from_ssh(
            paths,
            remote_host=args.remote_host,
            remote_path=args.remote_path,
            expected_sha256=args.expected_sha256,
            expected_size=args.expected_size,
            identity_file=args.identity_file,
            sudo_read=args.sudo_read,
            popen_factory=popen_factory,
        )
        payload: dict[str, int | str | bool] = manifest.as_dict()
    elif args.command == "import":
        from sqlalchemy import URL, create_engine

        from .migrations.postgres_baseline import create_postgres_baseline
        from .postgres_importer import import_sqlite_snapshot

        paths.assert_safe()
        integration_disk_report(paths)
        resolved_source = args.source.expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
        if resolved_source.parent != paths.snapshots:
            raise SnapshotVerificationError(
                "导入源必须位于项目 snapshots 目录"
            )
        load_snapshot_manifest(resolved_source)
        database_name = str(args.database).strip()
        if re.fullmatch(r"labelsystem_integration_[a-z0-9_]+", database_name) is None:
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                "本地导入数据库名必须使用 labelsystem_integration_ 前缀"
            )
        checkpoint_dir = (
            args.checkpoint_dir.expanduser().resolve(strict=False)
            if args.checkpoint_dir is not None
            else paths.checkpoints / database_name
        )
        if not checkpoint_dir.is_relative_to(paths.checkpoints):
            raise IntegrationPathError("导入检查点必须位于项目 checkpoints 目录")
        target = create_engine(
            URL.create(
                "postgresql+psycopg",
                username=str(args.database_user),
                database=database_name,
                query={"host": str(paths.socket_dir)},
            ),
            pool_pre_ping=True,
            hide_parameters=True,
        )
        try:
            with target.begin() as connection:
                create_postgres_baseline(connection)
            report = import_sqlite_snapshot(
                resolved_source,
                target,
                checkpoint_dir,
                batch_size=args.batch_size,
            )
        finally:
            target.dispose()
        payload = {
            "database": database_name,
            "source": str(report.source),
            "source_sha256": report.source_sha256,
            "inserted_rows": report.inserted_rows,
            "duplicate_rows": report.duplicate_rows,
            "completed_tables": report.completed_tables,
            "resumed": report.resumed,
            "checkpoint": str(
                checkpoint_dir / "sqlite-to-postgres-import.json"
            ),
        }
    elif args.command == "reconcile":
        from sqlalchemy import URL, create_engine

        from .postgres_reconciliation import (
            ReconciliationError,
            assert_critical_invariants,
            reconcile_databases,
        )

        paths.assert_safe()
        integration_disk_report(paths)
        resolved_source = args.source.expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
        if resolved_source.parent != paths.snapshots:
            raise SnapshotVerificationError(
                "对账源必须位于项目 snapshots 目录"
            )
        load_snapshot_manifest(resolved_source)
        database_name = str(args.database).strip()
        if re.fullmatch(r"labelsystem_integration_[a-z0-9_]+", database_name) is None:
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                "本地对账数据库名必须使用 labelsystem_integration_ 前缀"
            )
        report_name = (
            str(args.report_name).strip()
            if args.report_name
            else f"postgresql-reconciliation-{database_name}"
        )
        if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9._-]+", report_name) is None:
            raise IntegrationPathError("对账报告名包含不安全字符")
        target = create_engine(
            URL.create(
                "postgresql+psycopg",
                username=str(args.database_user),
                database=database_name,
                query={"host": str(paths.socket_dir)},
            ),
            pool_pre_ping=True,
            hide_parameters=True,
        )
        try:
            report = reconcile_databases(resolved_source, target)
        finally:
            target.dispose()
        accepted = True
        reconciliation_error = ""
        try:
            assert_critical_invariants(report)
        except ReconciliationError as exc:
            accepted = False
            reconciliation_error = str(exc)
        json_report = paths.reports / f"{report_name}.json"
        markdown_report = paths.reports / f"{report_name}.md"
        _atomic_write_text(
            json_report,
            json.dumps(
                {
                    "accepted": accepted,
                    "error": reconciliation_error,
                    **report.as_dict(),
                },
                ensure_ascii=False,
                indent=2,
                sort_keys=True,
            )
            + "\n",
            mode=0o600,
        )
        _atomic_write_text(
            markdown_report,
            report.to_markdown(
                accepted=accepted,
                error=reconciliation_error,
            ),
            mode=0o600,
        )
        _fsync_directory(paths.reports)
        if not accepted:
            raise PostgresIntegrationError(
                f"PostgreSQL 对账未通过；报告：{json_report}"
            )
        payload = {
            "database": database_name,
            "accepted": True,
            "tables": len(report.tables),
            "rows": sum(
                table.target_row_count for table in report.tables.values()
            ),
            "json_report": str(json_report),
            "markdown_report": str(markdown_report),
        }
    else:
        binaries = PostgresBinaries.for_prefix(args.postgres_prefix)
        payload = {}

    if args.command == "preflight":
        payload: dict[str, int | str | bool] = preflight_postgres(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
    elif args.command == "init":
        payload = preflight_postgres(
            paths,
            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
        initialize_postgres(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
        payload = {**payload, **postgres_status(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)}
    elif args.command == "start":
        preflight_postgres(
            paths,
