            binaries,
            filesystem_type=filesystem_type,
            run_command=run_command,
        )
        start_postgres(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
        payload = postgres_status(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
    elif args.command == "stop":
        stop_postgres(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
        payload = postgres_status(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)
    elif args.command == "status":
        payload = postgres_status(paths, binaries, run_command=run_command)

    print(
        json.dumps(
            {"command": args.command, "status": "ok", **payload},
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
        ),
        file=output,
    )
    return 0

===== FILE backend/app/postgres_reconciliation.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from urllib.parse import quote

from sqlalchemy import Engine, Integer, func, inspect, select, text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from .database import Base
from .migrations.postgres_invariants import INVARIANT_OWNERS
from .reconciliation_common import (
    CRITICAL_TABLES,
    ReconciliationError,
    TableReconciliation,
    canonical_database_value,
    schema_contract_compatible,
    table_digest as _table_digest,
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SequenceState:
    sequence_name: str
    primary_key_max: int | None
    last_value: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReconciliationReport:
    source: Path
    source_quick_check: str
    source_schema_version: int | None
    target_schema_version: int | None
    target_postgres_version: str
    source_foreign_key_violations: int
    target_foreign_key_violations: dict[str, int]
    schema_facts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]]
    tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation]
    sequences: dict[str, SequenceState]
    installed_invariant_triggers: tuple[str, ...]

    def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "source": str(self.source),
            "source_quick_check": self.source_quick_check,
            "source_schema_version": self.source_schema_version,
            "target_schema_version": self.target_schema_version,
            "target_postgres_version": self.target_postgres_version,
            "source_foreign_key_violations": (
                self.source_foreign_key_violations
            ),
            "target_foreign_key_violations": (
                self.target_foreign_key_violations
            ),
            "schema_facts": self.schema_facts,
            "tables": {
                name: {**asdict(table), "matches": table.matches}
                for name, table in self.tables.items()
            },
            "sequences": {
                name: asdict(state) for name, state in self.sequences.items()
            },
            "installed_invariant_triggers": list(
                self.installed_invariant_triggers
            ),
        }

    def to_markdown(self, *, accepted: bool, error: str = "") -> str:
        mismatches = [
            name for name, table in self.tables.items() if not table.matches
        ]
        target_fk_total = sum(self.target_foreign_key_violations.values())
        lines = [
            "# SQLite → PostgreSQL 对账报告",
            "",
            f"- 结论：{'通过' if accepted else '阻断'}",
            f"- 源快照：`{self.source}`",
            f"- 表：{len(self.tables)}",
            "- 源/目标业务行："
            f"{sum(item.source_row_count for item in self.tables.values())} / "
            f"{sum(item.target_row_count for item in self.tables.values())}",
            f"- 行级不一致表：{len(mismatches)}",
            f"- SQLite 外键违规：{self.source_foreign_key_violations}",
            f"- PostgreSQL 外键违规：{target_fk_total}",
            f"- PostgreSQL 不变量触发器：{len(self.installed_invariant_triggers)}",
            f"- Schema 版本：{self.source_schema_version} → {self.target_schema_version}",
        ]
        if mismatches:
            lines.extend(("", "## 不一致表", ""))
            lines.extend(f"- `{name}`" for name in mismatches)
        if error:
            lines.extend(("", "## 阻断原因", "", error))
        lines.append("")
        return "\n".join(lines)


def _sqlite_identifier(name: str) -> str:
    if not name.replace("_", "a").isalnum() or name[0].isdigit():
        raise ReconciliationError(f"不安全的 SQLite 标识符：{name}")
    return f'"{name}"'


def _source_rows(
    connection: sqlite3.Connection,
    table_name: str,
    columns: Sequence[Column[Any]],
    primary_key_name: str,
) -> Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    selected = ", ".join(_sqlite_identifier(column.name) for column in columns)
    cursor = connection.execute(
        f"SELECT {selected} FROM {_sqlite_identifier(table_name)} "
        f"ORDER BY {_sqlite_identifier(primary_key_name)}"
    )
    while rows := cursor.fetchmany(500):
        yield from rows


def _source_schema_version(connection: sqlite3.Connection) -> int | None:
    exists = connection.execute(
        "SELECT 1 FROM sqlite_master "
        "WHERE type='table' AND name='schema_migrations'"
    ).fetchone()
    if exists is None:
        return None
    value = connection.execute(
        "SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations"
    ).fetchone()[0]
    return None if value is None else int(value)


def _schema_facts(
    source: sqlite3.Connection,
    target: Connection,
) -> dict[str, dict[str, Any]]:
    target_inspector = inspect(target)
    facts: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        source_rows = source.execute(
            f"PRAGMA table_info({_sqlite_identifier(table.name)})"
        ).fetchall()
        source_columns = [str(row[1]) for row in source_rows]
        source_primary_key = [
            str(row[1]) for row in sorted(source_rows, key=lambda row: row[5]) if row[5]
        ]
        target_columns_info = target_inspector.get_columns(table.name)
        target_columns = [str(column["name"]) for column in target_columns_info]
        target_primary_key = list(
            target_inspector.get_pk_constraint(table.name).get(
                "constrained_columns"
            )
            or []
        )
        facts[table.name] = {
            "source_columns": source_columns,
            "target_columns": target_columns,
            "source_primary_key": source_primary_key,
            "target_primary_key": target_primary_key,
            "source_declared_types": {
                str(row[1]): str(row[2]) for row in source_rows
            },
            "target_declared_types": {
                str(column["name"]): str(column["type"])
                for column in target_columns_info
            },
            "compatible": schema_contract_compatible(
                source_columns=source_columns,
                target_columns=target_columns,
                source_primary_key=source_primary_key,
                target_primary_key=target_primary_key,
            ),
        }
    return facts


def _target_foreign_key_violations(
    connection: Connection,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    violations: dict[str, int] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        for constraint in sorted(
            table.foreign_key_constraints,
            key=lambda item: item.name or "",
        ):
            element = next(iter(constraint.elements))
            child = table.alias("child")
            parent = element.column.table.alias("parent")
            child_column = child.c[element.parent.name]
            parent_column = parent.c[element.column.name]
            key = (
                f"{table.name}.{element.parent.name}->"
                f"{element.column.table.name}.{element.column.name}"
            )
            count = connection.scalar(
                select(func.count())
                .select_from(
                    child.outerjoin(
                        parent,
                        child_column == parent_column,
                    )
                )
                .where(
                    child_column.is_not(None),
                    parent_column.is_(None),
                )
            )
            violations[key] = int(count or 0)
    return violations


def _sequence_states(
    connection: Connection,
    tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation],
) -> dict[str, SequenceState]:
    states: dict[str, SequenceState] = {}
    for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
        primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
        if not isinstance(primary_key.type, Integer):
            continue
        sequence_name = connection.scalar(
            text("SELECT pg_get_serial_sequence(:table_name, :column_name)"),
            {"table_name": table.name, "column_name": primary_key.name},
        )
        if sequence_name is None:
            continue
        sequence_short_name = str(sequence_name).rsplit(".", 1)[-1].strip('"')
        last_value = connection.scalar(
            text(
                "SELECT last_value FROM pg_sequences "
                "WHERE schemaname=current_schema() AND sequencename=:name"
            ),
            {"name": sequence_short_name},
        )
        maximum = tables[table.name].target_primary_key_max
        states[table.name] = SequenceState(
            sequence_name=str(sequence_name),
            primary_key_max=None if maximum is None else int(maximum),
            last_value=None if last_value is None else int(last_value),
        )
    return states


def reconcile_databases(source: Path, target: Engine) -> ReconciliationReport:
    """Stream and compare every mapped row without mutating either database."""
    from . import models as _models  # noqa: F401

    resolved_source = source.expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
    if source.expanduser().is_symlink() or not resolved_source.is_file():
        raise ReconciliationError("SQLite 对账源必须是普通文件且不得为符号链接")
    if target.dialect.name != "postgresql":
        raise ReconciliationError("对账目标必须是 PostgreSQL")
    source_connection = sqlite3.connect(
        f"file:{quote(str(resolved_source), safe='/')}?mode=ro&immutable=1",
        uri=True,
    )
    source_connection.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    source_connection.execute("PRAGMA query_only = ON")
    try:
        quick_check_rows = source_connection.execute(
            "PRAGMA quick_check"
        ).fetchall()
        source_quick_check = ",".join(str(row[0]) for row in quick_check_rows)
        source_fk_violations = sum(
            1 for _row in source_connection.execute("PRAGMA foreign_key_check")
        )
        source_version = _source_schema_version(source_connection)
        reconciled_tables: dict[str, TableReconciliation] = {}
        with target.connect() as target_connection:
            schema_facts = _schema_facts(
                source_connection,
                target_connection,
            )
            for table in Base.metadata.tables.values():
                columns = tuple(table.columns)
                primary_key = next(iter(table.primary_key.columns))
                source_values = _table_digest(
                    _source_rows(
                        source_connection,
                        table.name,
                        columns,
                        primary_key.name,
                    ),
                    table_name=table.name,
                    columns=columns,
                    primary_key_name=primary_key.name,
                )
                target_result = target_connection.execution_options(
                    stream_results=True,
                    yield_per=500,
                ).execute(select(table).order_by(primary_key))
                try:
                    target_values = _table_digest(
                        target_result.mappings(),
                        table_name=table.name,
                        columns=columns,
                        primary_key_name=primary_key.name,
                    )
                finally:
                    target_result.close()
                reconciled_tables[table.name] = TableReconciliation(
                    table_name=table.name,
                    source_row_count=source_values[0],
                    target_row_count=target_values[0],
                    source_primary_key_min=source_values[1],
                    source_primary_key_max=source_values[2],
                    target_primary_key_min=target_values[1],
                    target_primary_key_max=target_values[2],
                    source_null_counts=source_values[3],
                    target_null_counts=target_values[3],
                    source_hash=source_values[4],
                    target_hash=target_values[4],
                )
            target_fk_violations = _target_foreign_key_violations(
                target_connection
            )
            sequences = _sequence_states(
                target_connection,
                reconciled_tables,
            )
            target_schema_version = target_connection.scalar(
                text("SELECT max(version) FROM schema_migrations")
            )
            postgres_version = str(
                target_connection.scalar(text("SELECT version()"))
            )
            triggers = tuple(
                str(row[0])
                for row in target_connection.execute(
                    text(
                        "SELECT tgname FROM pg_trigger "
                        "WHERE NOT tgisinternal AND tgname LIKE 'trg_%' "
                        "ORDER BY tgname"
                    )
                )
            )
        return ReconciliationReport(
            source=resolved_source,
            source_quick_check=source_quick_check,
            source_schema_version=source_version,
            target_schema_version=(
                None
                if target_schema_version is None
                else int(target_schema_version)
            ),
            target_postgres_version=postgres_version,
            source_foreign_key_violations=source_fk_violations,
            target_foreign_key_violations=target_fk_violations,
            schema_facts=schema_facts,
            tables=reconciled_tables,
            sequences=sequences,
            installed_invariant_triggers=triggers,
        )
    finally:
        source_connection.close()


_CRITICAL_TABLES = CRITICAL_TABLES


def assert_critical_invariants(report: ReconciliationReport) -> None:
    failures: list[str] = []
    if report.source_quick_check != "ok":
        failures.append(
            f"SQLite quick_check={report.source_quick_check or 'empty'}"
        )
    if report.source_foreign_key_violations:
        failures.append(
            "SQLite foreign_key_check="
            f"{report.source_foreign_key_violations}"
        )
    target_fk_total = sum(report.target_foreign_key_violations.values())
    if target_fk_total:
        failures.append(f"PostgreSQL foreign_key_violations={target_fk_total}")
    incompatible_schema = [
        name
        for name, fact in report.schema_facts.items()
        if not fact["compatible"]
    ]
    if incompatible_schema:
        failures.append(
            "schema=" + ",".join(sorted(incompatible_schema)[:10])
        )
    mismatched_tables = [
        name for name, table in report.tables.items() if not table.matches
    ]
    if mismatched_tables:
        failures.append(
            "tables=" + ",".join(sorted(mismatched_tables)[:10])
        )
    missing_critical = _CRITICAL_TABLES - set(report.tables)
    if missing_critical:
        failures.append(
            "critical_missing=" + ",".join(sorted(missing_critical))
        )
    critical_mismatches = [
        name
        for name in _CRITICAL_TABLES & set(report.tables)
        if not report.tables[name].matches
    ]
    if critical_mismatches:
        failures.append(
            "critical=" + ",".join(sorted(critical_mismatches))
        )
    if (
        report.source_schema_version is not None
        and report.target_schema_version != report.source_schema_version
    ):
        failures.append(
            "schema_version="
            f"{report.source_schema_version}->{report.target_schema_version}"
        )
    expected_triggers = {
        name
        for name, owner in INVARIANT_OWNERS.items()
        if owner.kind == "postgres_trigger"
    }
    actual_triggers = set(report.installed_invariant_triggers)
    if actual_triggers != expected_triggers:
        failures.append(
            f"triggers={len(actual_triggers)}/{len(expected_triggers)}"
        )
    stale_sequences = [
        table_name
        for table_name, state in report.sequences.items()
        if state.primary_key_max is not None
        and state.last_value != state.primary_key_max
    ]
    if stale_sequences:
        failures.append(
            "sequences=" + ",".join(sorted(stale_sequences)[:10])
        )
    if failures:
        raise ReconciliationError("数据库对账未通过：" + "；".join(failures))

===== FILE backend/app/prepared_media_memo.py =====
"""Process-local memo for already-verified media preparation results.

2026-09-19 run 366: with 60 admitted slots the longest local phase reached
52 s while the host CPU stayed near 40 %. Every prompt call re-read each
frozen image (target plus up to 15 anchors) from the NAS three to four times
for sha256 checks and fully decoded it four times (geometry probe, geometry
cache validation, lossless pixel hash, lossless cache validation), even when
every derived file was already cached. Sixty slots doing that at once
saturated the NAS link, the admission controller read the slow phases as
local pressure and halved itself down to 8.

The memo remembers, per worker process, the verified outcome for one exact
input identity (path, mtime_ns, size, declared sha256, policy key) together
with the derived file's identity. A later call with the same identities
returns the same outcome without touching the pixels again; any change to
either file (mtime or size) drops the entry and the full verification runs
again. Evidence produced from a memo hit is byte-identical to a cache hit.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import threading
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Hashable


def file_identity(path: Path) -> tuple[str, int, int] | None:
    """Return (path, mtime_ns, size) for a regular file, else None."""

    try:
        stat_result = path.stat()
    except OSError:
        return None
    return (str(path), stat_result.st_mtime_ns, stat_result.st_size)


class BoundedMemo:
    """Small thread-safe FIFO memo; a full memo drops its oldest quarter."""

    def __init__(self, max_entries: int) -> None:
        if max_entries < 4:
            raise ValueError("memo needs at least four entries")
        self._max_entries = max_entries
        self._entries: dict[Hashable, Any] = {}
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    def get(self, key: Hashable) -> Any:
        with self._lock:
            return self._entries.get(key)

    def put(self, key: Hashable, value: Any) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            if key not in self._entries and len(self._entries) >= self._max_entries:
                for stale in list(self._entries)[: self._max_entries // 4]:
                    self._entries.pop(stale, None)
            self._entries[key] = value

    def discard(self, key: Hashable) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._entries.pop(key, None)

    def clear(self) -> None:
        with self._lock:
            self._entries.clear()

    def __len__(self) -> int:
        with self._lock:
            return len(self._entries)


_DIGEST_MEMO = BoundedMemo(8192)


def sha256_file_memo(path: Path, compute) -> str:
    """Digest a file once per (path, mtime_ns, size); recompute on any change."""

    identity = file_identity(path)
    if identity is None:
        return compute(path)
    cached = _DIGEST_MEMO.get(identity)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached
    digest = compute(path)
    _DIGEST_MEMO.put(identity, digest)
    return digest

===== FILE backend/app/production_dimension_contract.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .dimension_schema_registry import canonical_hash
from .models import DimensionSchema, StrategyBundle
from .scoring import (
    DimensionScoringContractError,
    validate_dimension_scoring_contract,
)


class ProductionDimensionContractError(ValueError):
    """A category dimension contract is unsafe for production execution."""

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.technical_error_type = code
        self.retryable = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ProductionDimensionContract:
    schema_id: int
    schema_key: str
    version: str
    canonical_hash: str
    definition: dict[str, Any]


def resolve_published_dimension_contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    schema_key: str | None,
    version: str | None,
    bundle: StrategyBundle | None = None,
    require_configured: bool = False,
) -> ProductionDimensionContract | None:
    """Resolve one immutable, published schema and optionally verify its bundle copy."""
    if schema_key is None and version is None:
        if not require_configured:
            return None
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_incomplete",
            "类目尚未配置生产维度方案，请选择已发布版本。",
        )
    if not schema_key or not version:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_incomplete",
            "类目维度合同不完整，请同时配置维度方案和版本。",
        )

    matches = db.scalars(
        select(DimensionSchema).where(
            DimensionSchema.schema_key == schema_key,
            DimensionSchema.version == version,
        )
    ).all()
    if not matches:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_missing",
            "类目绑定的维度方案不存在，请重新选择已发布版本。",
        )
    if len(matches) != 1:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_ambiguous",
            "类目绑定的维度方案存在重复版本，已停止生产执行。",
        )
    schema = matches[0]
    if schema.status != "published":
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_not_published",
            "类目绑定的维度方案尚未发布或已停用，请选择现役发布版本。",
        )
    try:
        definition = json.loads(schema.definition_json)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_invalid",
            "类目绑定的维度方案内容损坏，已停止生产执行。",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(definition, dict) or canonical_hash(definition) != schema.canonical_hash:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_invalid",
            "类目绑定的维度方案校验失败，已停止生产执行。",
        )

    try:
        validate_dimension_scoring_contract(definition)
    except (DimensionScoringContractError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_not_executable",
            "类目绑定的维度方案与当前评分引擎不兼容，已停止生产执行。",
        ) from exc

    if bundle is not None:
        try:
            frozen_set = json.loads(bundle.dimension_schema_set_snapshot or "")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 冻结维度合同损坏，已停止生产执行。",
            ) from exc
        entries = frozen_set.get("schemas") if isinstance(frozen_set, dict) else None
        frozen_matches = [
            entry
            for entry in entries or []
            if isinstance(entry, dict)
            and entry.get("schema_key") == schema_key
            and entry.get("version") == version
        ]
        if len(frozen_matches) != 1:
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 未唯一冻结类目现役维度方案，已停止生产执行。",
            )
        frozen = frozen_matches[0]
        if (
            frozen.get("canonical_hash") != schema.canonical_hash
            or frozen.get("definition") != definition
        ):
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 冻结维度方案与注册表不一致，已停止生产执行。",
            )
    return ProductionDimensionContract(
        schema_id=schema.id,
        schema_key=schema.schema_key,
        version=schema.version,
        canonical_hash=schema.canonical_hash,
        definition=definition,
    )


def resolve_frozen_dimension_contract(
    snapshot: dict[str, Any],
    *,
    bundle: StrategyBundle | None = None,
) -> ProductionDimensionContract | None:
    """Validate a dimension contract frozen when a job was accepted."""
    frozen = snapshot.get("dimension_contract")
    if frozen is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(frozen, dict):
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_invalid",
            "任务冻结维度合同损坏，已停止生产执行。",
        )
    definition = frozen.get("definition")
    schema_key = frozen.get("schema_key")
    version = frozen.get("version")
    schema_hash = frozen.get("canonical_hash")
    schema_id = frozen.get("schema_id")
    if (
        not isinstance(schema_id, int)
        or not isinstance(schema_key, str)
        or not isinstance(version, str)
        or not isinstance(schema_hash, str)
        or not isinstance(definition, dict)
        or canonical_hash(definition) != schema_hash
    ):
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_invalid",
            "任务冻结维度合同校验失败，已停止生产执行。",
        )
    try:
        validate_dimension_scoring_contract(definition)
    except (DimensionScoringContractError, TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
            "dimension_contract_not_executable",
            "任务冻结维度合同与当前评分引擎不兼容，已停止生产执行。",
        ) from exc
    if bundle is not None:
        try:
            frozen_set = json.loads(bundle.dimension_schema_set_snapshot or "")
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 冻结维度合同损坏，已停止生产执行。",
            ) from exc
        entries = frozen_set.get("schemas") if isinstance(frozen_set, dict) else None
        matches = [
            entry for entry in entries or []
            if isinstance(entry, dict)
            and entry.get("schema_key") == schema_key
            and entry.get("version") == version
            and entry.get("canonical_hash") == schema_hash
            and entry.get("definition") == definition
        ]
        if len(matches) != 1:
            raise ProductionDimensionContractError(
                "dimension_contract_not_executable",
                "StrategyBundle 与任务冻结维度合同不一致，已停止生产执行。",
            )
    return ProductionDimensionContract(
        schema_id=schema_id,
        schema_key=schema_key,
        version=version,
        canonical_hash=schema_hash,
        definition=definition,
    )

===== FILE backend/app/production_feedback.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import append_audit_event, canonical_json
from .models import EvaluationCategoryProfile, OptimizationCaseQueue, ProductionFeedbackEvent


SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION = "production-feedback-v1"
SUPPORTED_EVENT_TYPE = "human_correction_finalized"


class FeedbackConflict(ValueError):
    pass


def ingest_production_feedback(
    db: Session,
    *,
    event_id: str,
    schema_version: str,
    event_type: str,
    source_system: str,
    occurred_at: datetime,
    payload: dict[str, Any],
    received_by: str,
) -> tuple[ProductionFeedbackEvent, OptimizationCaseQueue, bool]:
    if schema_version != SUPPORTED_SCHEMA_VERSION:
        raise ValueError("不支持的生产反馈 schema_version")
    if event_type != SUPPORTED_EVENT_TYPE:
        raise ValueError("不支持的生产反馈 event_type")
    required = {
        "production_case_id",
        "category_key",
        "prompt_version",
        "severity",
        "model_output",
        "human_truth",
    }
    if not required.issubset(payload):
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 payload 缺少必填字段")
    if payload["severity"] not in {"P0", "P1", "P2", "P3"}:
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 severity 非法")
    if not isinstance(payload["model_output"], dict) or not isinstance(
        payload["human_truth"], dict
    ):
        raise ValueError("生产反馈模型结果与人工真值必须为对象")
    prompt_version = str(payload["prompt_version"]).strip()
    if not prompt_version or len(prompt_version) > 40:
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 prompt_version 非法")
    projected_label_version = payload.get("projected_label_version")
    if projected_label_version is not None and (
        isinstance(projected_label_version, bool)
        or not isinstance(projected_label_version, int)
        or projected_label_version < 1
    ):
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 projected_label_version 必须为正整数")
    shadow_batches = payload.get("shadow_batches")
    normalized_shadow_batches: list[str] | None = None
    if shadow_batches is not None:
        if not isinstance(shadow_batches, list) or len(shadow_batches) > 20:
            raise ValueError("生产反馈 shadow_batches 必须是不超过 20 项的数组")
        normalized_shadow_batches = []
        for batch_id in shadow_batches:
            if not isinstance(batch_id, str):
                raise ValueError("生产反馈 shadow_batches 只能包含字符串")
            normalized_batch_id = batch_id.strip()
            if not normalized_batch_id or len(normalized_batch_id) > 160:
                raise ValueError("生产反馈 shadow_batches 包含非法批次标识")
            if normalized_batch_id in normalized_shadow_batches:
                raise ValueError("生产反馈 shadow_batches 不能包含重复批次")
            normalized_shadow_batches.append(normalized_batch_id)
    category_key = str(payload["category_key"]).strip()
    profile = db.scalar(
        select(EvaluationCategoryProfile).where(
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key == category_key,
            EvaluationCategoryProfile.status == "active",
        )
    )
    if profile is None:
        raise ValueError("生产反馈 category_key 非法")

    payload_json = canonical_json(payload)
    payload_hash = hashlib.sha256(payload_json.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ProductionFeedbackEvent).where(
            ProductionFeedbackEvent.event_id == event_id
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        existing_occurred = existing.occurred_at
        if existing_occurred.tzinfo is None:
            existing_occurred = existing_occurred.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        incoming_occurred = occurred_at
        if incoming_occurred.tzinfo is None:
            incoming_occurred = incoming_occurred.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
        if (
            existing.payload_hash != payload_hash
            or existing.schema_version != schema_version
            or existing.event_type != event_type
            or existing.source_system != source_system
            or existing_occurred != incoming_occurred
        ):
            raise FeedbackConflict("同一 event_id 的事件载荷不一致")
        existing_case = db.scalar(
            select(OptimizationCaseQueue).where(
                OptimizationCaseQueue.source_event_id == existing.id
            )
        )
        if existing_case is None:
            raise RuntimeError("生产反馈事件缺少优化队列映射")
        return existing, existing_case, True

    event = ProductionFeedbackEvent(
        event_id=event_id,
        schema_version=schema_version,
        event_type=event_type,
        source_system=source_system,
        occurred_at=occurred_at,
        payload_hash=payload_hash,
        payload_json=payload_json,
        status="mapped",
        received_by=received_by,
    )
    db.add(event)
    db.flush()
    case_payload = {
        "schema_version": "optimization-case-v1",
        "source": "production_feedback",
        "source_event_id": event_id,
        "production_case_id": payload["production_case_id"],
        "model_output": payload["model_output"],
        "human_truth": payload["human_truth"],
        "reason_codes": payload.get("reason_codes", []),
        "production_applied": bool(payload.get("production_applied", False)),
        "writes_production_database": False,
    }
    if projected_label_version is not None:
        case_payload["projected_label_version"] = projected_label_version
    if normalized_shadow_batches is not None:
        case_payload["shadow_batches"] = normalized_shadow_batches
    case = OptimizationCaseQueue(
        category_key=category_key,
        idempotency_key=f"production:{event_id}",
        evaluation_id=None,
        final_review_id=None,
        source_type="production_feedback",
        source_event_id=event.id,
        prompt_version=prompt_version,
        severity=str(payload["severity"]),
        case_json=canonical_json(case_payload),
        status="pending",
    )
    db.add(case)
    db.flush()
    append_audit_event(
        db,
        category="production_feedback",
        action="accepted",
        subject_type="production_feedback_event",
        subject_id=event.event_id,
        actor=received_by,
        payload={
            "payload_hash": payload_hash,
            "optimization_case_id": case.id,
            "writes_production_database": False,
        },
        event_key=f"production-feedback:{event_id}",
    )
    return event, case, False

===== FILE backend/app/production_title_normalization.py =====
"""Versioned 3DSU title formatting; never rewrite visual facts or scores."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from collections.abc import Mapping
from typing import Any

PROFILE = "3dsu-chinese-title-preserve-latin-v1"
CONFIG_KEY = "production_title_normalization_profile"
# Keep complete Latin phrases (including brand spelling, spaces and digits).
_LATIN = re.compile(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9]*(?:[ '\u2019&.-]+[A-Za-z0-9]+)*")
_CHINESE = re.compile(r"[\u3400-\u4dbf\u4e00-\u9fff]+")


def frozen_title_profile(node: Mapping[str, Any], category_key: str) -> str | None:
    config = node.get("config") or {}
    profile = config.get(CONFIG_KEY)
    if profile is None:
        return None
    if (profile != PROFILE or category_key != "model_3d_su"
            or node.get("node_instance_id") != "prompt_a"
            or (config.get("output_contract") or {}).get("name") != "model_3d_su_call_a"):
        raise ValueError("标题规范策略只允许冻结到 3DSU 调用 A 的标准合同")
    return profile


def normalize_production_title(contract, parsed: dict[str, Any], profile: str | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Apply an atomic, idempotent format repair and return bounded audit facts.

    Only remove Latin text when the remaining title is a complete Chinese
    string of 1..10 characters. Never truncate, translate, invent a subject,
    drop numbers/punctuation, replace tags or relax the frozen contract.
    Raw provider text/payload remain untouched in DoubaoResponse.
    """
    if profile is None:
        return []
    if profile != PROFILE or contract.name != "model_3d_su_call_a":
        raise ValueError("Unsupported production title normalization profile")
    fields = parsed.get("production_fields")
    if not isinstance(fields, dict):
        return []
    title, seo, tags = (fields.get(key) for key in ("title", "seotitle", "tags"))
    title_schema = contract.schema.get("properties", {}).get("production_fields", {}).get("properties", {}).get("title", {})
    if (title_schema.get("maxLength") != 10 or not isinstance(title, str)
            or not 10 < len(title) <= 80 or not isinstance(seo, str)
            or not isinstance(tags, list) or not all(isinstance(tag, str) for tag in tags)):
        return []
    phrases = list(dict.fromkeys(match.group() for match in _LATIN.finditer(title)))
    shorter = _LATIN.sub("", title).strip()
    if not phrases or not 1 <= len(shorter) <= 10 or not _CHINESE.fullmatch(shorter):
        return []
    updated_tags = list(tags)
    preserved = []
    for phrase in phrases:
        if phrase in seo:
            location = "production_fields.seotitle"
        else:
            index = next((i for i, tag in enumerate(updated_tags) if phrase in tag), None)
            if index is None:
                index = len(updated_tags)
                updated_tags.append(phrase)
            location = f"production_fields.tags[{index}]"
        preserved.append({"text": phrase, "field": location})
    trace = [{"profile": PROFILE, "field": "production_fields.title",
              "before": title, "after": shorter, "preserved_text": preserved}]
    if updated_tags != tags:
        trace.append({"profile": PROFILE, "field": "production_fields.tags",
                      "before": list(tags), "after": updated_tags})
    fields["title"] = shorter
    fields["tags"] = updated_tags
    return trace

===== FILE backend/app/projection_contracts.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Mapping

from sqlalchemy import delete, select
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from .audit import canonical_json
from .models import (
    LocalProjectionRow,
    ProjectionContract,
    ProjectionManifest,
    ProjectionReconciliation,
    PublishedLabel,
)


BUILTIN_LOCAL_TARGETS = {
    "unified_dimension": "unified_dimension_table",
    "search_labels": "search_labels_small_table",
    "quality_governance": "quality_governance_small_table",
}

_ALLOWED_ROOTS = {
    "content_key",
    "category_key",
    "level",
    "score",
    "classification",
    "dimensions",
    "production_fields",
    "image_quality",
    "media_form",
    "semantic",
    "quality",
    "governance",
}
_ALLOWED_PROVENANCE = {
    "asset_version_id",
    "asset_id",
    "asset_sha256",
    "asset_scope",
    "is_single",
    "evaluation_id",
    "final_review_id",
    "strategy_bundle_id",
    "model_id",
    "prompt_a_version",
    "prompt_b_version",
    "rubric_version",
    "engine_version",
    "tag_contract_version",
    "normalization_version",
    "mapping_version",
}
_ALLOWED_LABEL_META = {
    "$label.id",
    "$label.release_id",
    "$label.version",
    "$label.schema_version",
    "$label.payload_hash",
    "$label.published_at",
}
_FORBIDDEN_TOKENS = {
    "raw_response",
    "candidate",
    "human_review",
    "review",
    "credential",
    "api_key",
    "password",
    "token",
    "secret",
    "manual_process",
}


class ProjectionContractError(ValueError):
    pass


def _hash(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(value).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()


def validate_projection_contract(
    *,
    target_role: str,
    table_name: str,
    environment: str,
    primary_key: list[str],
    field_mappings: Mapping[str, str],
    write_policy: str = "local_only",
    target_key: str | None = None,
    category_key: str | None = None,
    field_contract_id: int | None = None,
    max_batch_size: int = 500,
    adapter_key: str = "local-sqlite",
) -> None:
    if environment == "shadow":
        if (
            write_policy != "shadow_only"
            or not target_key
            or not category_key
            or not field_contract_id
            or field_contract_id < 1
            or not adapter_key.strip()
        ):
            raise ProjectionContractError(
                "影子投影必须绑定目标、类目、字段合同和 shadow_only 策略"
            )
        if not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*", table_name):
            raise ProjectionContractError("影子表名必须是已校验标识符")
    else:
        expected_table = BUILTIN_LOCAL_TARGETS.get(target_role)
        if expected_table is None or table_name != expected_table:
            raise ProjectionContractError("仅支持已登记的本地大维表和小表目标")
        if environment not in {"local", "test"} or write_policy != "local_only":
            raise ProjectionContractError("本地投影只允许 local/test 与 local_only")
    if not 1 <= max_batch_size <= 500:
        raise ProjectionContractError("投影批次必须在 1 到 500 条之间")
    if primary_key != ["content_key"]:
        raise ProjectionContractError("本地投影主键必须为 content_key")
    if not field_mappings or field_mappings.get("content_key") != "content_key":
        raise ProjectionContractError("字段映射必须包含 content_key")
    for target_field, source_path in field_mappings.items():
        if not target_field.strip() or not source_path.strip():
            raise ProjectionContractError("字段映射不能为空")
        if source_path in _ALLOWED_LABEL_META:
            continue
        root, _, suffix = source_path.partition(".")
        if root == "provenance" and suffix in _ALLOWED_PROVENANCE:
            continue
        normalized = f"{target_field}.{source_path}".lower()
        if any(token in normalized for token in _FORBIDDEN_TOKENS):
            raise ProjectionContractError(
                f"禁止将候选、凭据、模型原始响应或人工过程字段投影：{source_path}"
            )
        if root in _ALLOWED_ROOTS:
            continue
        raise ProjectionContractError(
            f"禁止将候选、凭据、模型原始响应或人工过程字段投影：{source_path}"
        )


def contract_payload(contract: ProjectionContract) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "version": contract.version,
        "target_role": contract.target_role,
        "table_name": contract.table_name,
        "environment": contract.environment,
        "adapter_key": contract.adapter_key,
        "target_key": contract.target_key,
        "write_policy": contract.write_policy,
        "category_key": contract.category_key,
        "field_contract_id": contract.field_contract_id,
        "max_batch_size": contract.max_batch_size,
        "primary_key": json.loads(contract.primary_key_json),
        "field_mappings": json.loads(contract.field_mappings_json),
        "input_versions": json.loads(contract.input_versions_json),
        "mode": contract.mode,
        "idempotency_key_template": contract.idempotency_key_template,
        "checkpoint": json.loads(contract.checkpoint_json),
        "reconciliation": json.loads(contract.reconciliation_json),
        "rollback": json.loads(contract.rollback_json),
        "owner": contract.owner,
        "status": contract.status,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "created_by": contract.created_by,
        "created_at": contract.created_at,
    }


def create_contract_version(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract_key: str,
    target_role: str,
    table_name: str,
    environment: str,
    primary_key: list[str],
    field_mappings: Mapping[str, str],
    input_versions: Mapping[str, Any],
    mode: str,
    idempotency_key_template: str,
    checkpoint: Mapping[str, Any],
    reconciliation: Mapping[str, Any],
    rollback: Mapping[str, Any],
    owner: str,
    status: str,
    created_by: str,
    adapter_key: str = "local-sqlite",
    target_key: str | None = None,
    write_policy: str = "local_only",
    category_key: str | None = None,
    field_contract_id: int | None = None,
    max_batch_size: int = 500,
) -> ProjectionContract:
    validate_projection_contract(
        target_role=target_role,
        table_name=table_name,
        environment=environment,
        primary_key=primary_key,
        field_mappings=field_mappings,
        write_policy=write_policy,
        target_key=target_key,
        category_key=category_key,
        field_contract_id=field_contract_id,
        max_batch_size=max_batch_size,
        adapter_key=adapter_key,
    )
    latest = db.scalar(
        select(ProjectionContract)
        .where(ProjectionContract.contract_key == contract_key)
        .order_by(ProjectionContract.version.desc())
        .limit(1)
    )
    version = (latest.version + 1) if latest else 1
    definition = {
        "contract_key": contract_key,
        "version": version,
        "target_role": target_role,
        "table_name": table_name,
        "environment": environment,
        "adapter_key": adapter_key,
        "target_key": target_key,
        "write_policy": write_policy,
        "category_key": category_key,
        "field_contract_id": field_contract_id,
        "max_batch_size": max_batch_size,
        "primary_key": primary_key,
        "field_mappings": dict(field_mappings),
        "input_versions": dict(input_versions),
        "mode": mode,
        "idempotency_key_template": idempotency_key_template,
        "checkpoint": dict(checkpoint),
        "reconciliation": dict(reconciliation),
        "rollback": dict(rollback),
        "owner": owner,
        "status": status,
    }
    contract = ProjectionContract(
        contract_key=contract_key,
        version=version,
        target_role=target_role,
        table_name=table_name,
        environment=environment,
        adapter_key=adapter_key,
        target_key=target_key,
        write_policy=write_policy,
        category_key=category_key,
        field_contract_id=field_contract_id,
        max_batch_size=max_batch_size,
        primary_key_json=canonical_json(primary_key),
        field_mappings_json=canonical_json(dict(field_mappings)),
        input_versions_json=canonical_json(dict(input_versions)),
        mode=mode,
        idempotency_key_template=idempotency_key_template,
        checkpoint_json=canonical_json(dict(checkpoint)),
        reconciliation_json=canonical_json(dict(reconciliation)),
        rollback_json=canonical_json(dict(rollback)),
        owner=owner,
        status=status,
        contract_hash=_hash(definition),
        created_by=created_by,
    )
    db.add(contract)
    db.flush()
    return contract


def _lookup(payload: Mapping[str, Any], path: str) -> Any:
    parts = path.split(".")
    if len(parts) == 4 and parts[0] == "semantic" and parts[2] in {"primary_name", "weighted_names"}:
        field = payload.get("semantic")
        field_payload = field.get(parts[1]) if isinstance(field, Mapping) else None
        values = field_payload.get("values") if isinstance(field_payload, Mapping) else None
        if not isinstance(values, list):
            return "" if parts[2] == "primary_name" else ""
        locale = parts[3]
        rendered: list[tuple[str, float | None]] = []
        for item in values:
            if not isinstance(item, Mapping):
                continue
            names = item.get("localized_names") or item.get("names") or {}
            name = names.get(locale) if isinstance(names, Mapping) else None
            name = str(name or item.get("value") or "").strip()
            if name:
                weight = item.get("weight")
                rendered.append((name, float(weight) if isinstance(weight, (int, float)) and not isinstance(weight, bool) else None))
        if parts[2] == "primary_name":
            return rendered[0][0] if rendered else ""
        return ",".join(
            f"{name}_{weight:g}" if weight is not None else name
            for name, weight in rendered
        )
    if path == "provenance.is_single":
        provenance = payload.get("provenance")
        if isinstance(provenance, Mapping) and provenance.get("is_single") is not None:
            return int(bool(provenance["is_single"]))
        return 1 if isinstance(provenance, Mapping) and provenance.get("asset_scope") == "single" else 0
    value: Any = payload
    for part in path.split("."):
        if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
            return None
        value = value.get(part)
    return value


def _label_meta(label: PublishedLabel, source_path: str) -> Any:
    return {
        "$label.id": label.id,
        "$label.release_id": label.release_id,
        "$label.version": label.version,
        "$label.schema_version": label.label_schema_version,
        "$label.payload_hash": label.payload_hash,
        "$label.published_at": label.published_at.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat()
        if label.published_at.tzinfo
        else label.published_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    }[source_path]


def _projection_row(
    label: PublishedLabel,
    *,
    field_mappings: Mapping[str, str],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload = json.loads(label.label_payload_json or "{}")
    row = {
        target: (
            _label_meta(label, source)
            if source.startswith("$label.")
            else _lookup(payload, source)
        )
        for target, source in sorted(field_mappings.items())
    }
    row["_projection_version"] = {
        "published_label_id": label.id,
        "label_version": label.version,
    }
    return row


def _manifest_payload(
    contract: ProjectionContract,
    *,
    rows: list[dict[str, Any]],
    labels: list[PublishedLabel],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload_hash = _hash(rows)
    provenance = [json.loads(label.label_payload_json or "{}").get("provenance") or {} for label in labels]
    input_versions = {
        "label_schema_versions": sorted({label.label_schema_version for label in labels}),
        "label_release_versions": sorted({label.version for label in labels}),
        "asset_versions": sorted(
            {str(item.get("asset_version_id") or item.get("asset_sha256") or item.get("asset_id")) for item in provenance if item.get("asset_version_id") or item.get("asset_sha256") or item.get("asset_id")}
        ),
        "mechanism_versions": sorted(
            {f"strategy-bundle:{item['strategy_bundle_id']}" for item in provenance if item.get("strategy_bundle_id") is not None}
        ),
        "model_versions": sorted(
            {str(item["model_id"]) for item in provenance if item.get("model_id")}
        ),
        "tag_contract_versions": sorted(
            {str(item["tag_contract_version"]) for item in provenance if item.get("tag_contract_version")}
        ),
        "mapping_versions": sorted(
            {str(item["mapping_version"]) for item in provenance if item.get("mapping_version")}
        ),
    }
    base = {
        "schema_version": "projection-manifest-v1",
        "contract_id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "contract_version": contract.version,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "target_role": contract.target_role,
        "target_table": contract.table_name,
        "environment": contract.environment,
        "mode": contract.mode,
        "row_count": len(rows),
        "content_keys": [str(row["content_key"]) for row in rows],
        "payload_hash": payload_hash,
        "input_versions": input_versions,
        "rows": rows,
    }
    return {**base, "manifest_hash": _hash(base)}


def build_projection_manifest(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract: ProjectionContract,
) -> tuple[ProjectionManifest, dict[str, Any]]:
    mappings = json.loads(contract.field_mappings_json)
    labels = list(
        db.scalars(
            select(PublishedLabel)
            .where(PublishedLabel.status == "published")
            .order_by(PublishedLabel.content_key.asc(), PublishedLabel.id.asc())
        ).all()
    )
    rows = [_projection_row(label, field_mappings=mappings) for label in labels]
    payload = _manifest_payload(contract, rows=rows, labels=labels)
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(ProjectionManifest).where(
            ProjectionManifest.contract_id == contract.id,
            ProjectionManifest.manifest_hash == payload["manifest_hash"],
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing, payload
    manifest = ProjectionManifest(
        contract_id=contract.id,
        manifest_hash=payload["manifest_hash"],
        payload_hash=payload["payload_hash"],
        row_count=payload["row_count"],
        content_keys_json=canonical_json(payload["content_keys"]),
        input_versions_json=canonical_json(payload["input_versions"]),
        rows_json=canonical_json(rows),
    )
    db.add(manifest)
    db.flush()
    return manifest, payload


def manifest_payload(manifest: ProjectionManifest, contract: ProjectionContract) -> dict[str, Any]:
    rows = json.loads(manifest.rows_json)
    return {
        "id": manifest.id,
        "schema_version": "projection-manifest-v1",
        "contract_id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "contract_version": contract.version,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "target_role": contract.target_role,
        "target_table": contract.table_name,
        "environment": contract.environment,
        "mode": contract.mode,
        "row_count": manifest.row_count,
        "content_keys": json.loads(manifest.content_keys_json),
        "payload_hash": manifest.payload_hash,
        "manifest_hash": manifest.manifest_hash,
        "input_versions": json.loads(manifest.input_versions_json),
        "rows": rows,
        "created_at": manifest.created_at,
    }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ReconciliationResult:
    status: str
    reason: str
    row_count: int
    missing_count: int
    unexpected_count: int
    payload_hash: str
    version_match: bool


class LocalProjectionAdapter:
    def apply(
        self,
        db: Session,
        *,
        contract: ProjectionContract,
        manifest: ProjectionManifest,
    ) -> None:
        rows = json.loads(manifest.rows_json)
        expected_keys = {str(row["content_key"]) for row in rows}
        existing = {
            row.content_key: row
            for row in db.scalars(
                select(LocalProjectionRow).where(
                    LocalProjectionRow.table_name == contract.table_name
                )
            ).all()
        }
        for row in rows:
            content_key = str(row["content_key"])
            version = row.get("_projection_version") or {}
            raw = canonical_json(row)
            stored = existing.get(content_key)
            if stored is None:
                stored = LocalProjectionRow(
                    table_name=contract.table_name,
                    content_key=content_key,
                    contract_id=contract.id,
                    contract_version=contract.version,
                    published_label_id=int(version["published_label_id"]),
                    label_version=int(version["label_version"]),
                    payload_json=raw,
                    payload_hash=_hash(row),
                )
                db.add(stored)
            else:
                stored.contract_id = contract.id
                stored.contract_version = contract.version
                stored.published_label_id = int(version["published_label_id"])
                stored.label_version = int(version["label_version"])
                stored.payload_json = raw
                stored.payload_hash = _hash(row)
                stored.updated_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        if contract.mode == "snapshot":
            stale_keys = set(existing) - expected_keys
            if stale_keys:
                db.execute(
                    delete(LocalProjectionRow).where(
                        LocalProjectionRow.table_name == contract.table_name,
                        LocalProjectionRow.content_key.in_(stale_keys),
                    )
                )
        db.flush()

    def reconcile(
        self,
        db: Session,
        *,
        contract: ProjectionContract,
        manifest: ProjectionManifest,
    ) -> ReconciliationResult:
        expected_rows = json.loads(manifest.rows_json)
        actual_models = list(
            db.scalars(
                select(LocalProjectionRow)
                .where(LocalProjectionRow.table_name == contract.table_name)
                .order_by(LocalProjectionRow.content_key.asc())
            ).all()
        )
        actual_rows = [json.loads(row.payload_json) for row in actual_models]
        expected_keys = {str(row["content_key"]) for row in expected_rows}
        actual_keys = {row.content_key for row in actual_models}
        missing = expected_keys - actual_keys
        unexpected = actual_keys - expected_keys
        actual_hash = _hash(actual_rows)
        version_match = all(
            row.contract_id == contract.id and row.contract_version == contract.version
            for row in actual_models
        )
        reason = ""
        if missing:
            reason = "missing_rows"
        elif unexpected:
            reason = "unexpected_rows"
        elif actual_hash != manifest.payload_hash:
            reason = "payload_hash_mismatch"
        elif not version_match:
            reason = "version_mismatch"
        return ReconciliationResult(
            status="matched" if not reason else "drift",
            reason=reason,
            row_count=len(actual_rows),
            missing_count=len(missing),
            unexpected_count=len(unexpected),
            payload_hash=actual_hash,
            version_match=version_match,
        )


def persist_reconciliation(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract: ProjectionContract,
    manifest: ProjectionManifest,
    result: ReconciliationResult,
) -> ProjectionReconciliation:
    record = ProjectionReconciliation(
        contract_id=contract.id,
        manifest_id=manifest.id,
        target_table=contract.table_name,
        status=result.status,
        reason=result.reason,
        row_count=result.row_count,
        missing_count=result.missing_count,
        unexpected_count=result.unexpected_count,
        expected_payload_hash=manifest.payload_hash,
        actual_payload_hash=result.payload_hash,
        version_match=result.version_match,
        checkpoint_json=contract.checkpoint_json,
        compensation_json=canonical_json(
            {
                "retryable": result.status != "matched",
                "strategy": "rebuild_from_published_labels",
                "canonical_rows_mutated": False,
            }
        ),
    )
    db.add(record)
    db.flush()
    return record


def reconciliation_payload(record: ProjectionReconciliation) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "id": record.id,
        "contract_id": record.contract_id,
        "manifest_id": record.manifest_id,
        "target_table": record.target_table,
        "status": record.status,
        "reason": record.reason,
        "row_count": record.row_count,
        "missing_count": record.missing_count,
        "unexpected_count": record.unexpected_count,
        "expected_payload_hash": record.expected_payload_hash,
        "payload_hash": record.actual_payload_hash,
        "version_match": record.version_match,
        "checkpoint": json.loads(record.checkpoint_json),
        "compensation": json.loads(record.compensation_json),
        "created_at": record.created_at,
    }

===== FILE backend/app/prompt_contract_merge.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from copy import deepcopy
import hashlib
from typing import Any

from .audit import canonical_json


class PromptContractMergeError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, path: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.path = path


def _invalid(message: str, *, path: str = "$") -> None:
    raise PromptContractMergeError(
        "prompt_contract_invalid",
        message,
        path=path,
    )


def _conflict(path: str) -> None:
    raise PromptContractMergeError(
        "prompt_contract_field_conflict",
        f"严格输出合同在 {path} 的字段语义冲突",
        path=path,
    )


def _canonical_mapping(value: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    normalized: dict[str, Any] = {}
    for key in sorted(value):
        child = value[key]
        if isinstance(child, Mapping):
            normalized[key] = _canonical_mapping(child)
        elif isinstance(child, list):
            normalized[key] = [
                _canonical_mapping(item) if isinstance(item, Mapping) else deepcopy(item)
                for item in child
            ]
        else:
            normalized[key] = deepcopy(child)
    return normalized


def _normalize_schema(schema: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if schema.get("type") == "object":
        meta = {
            key: value
            for key, value in schema.items()
            if key not in {"properties", "required", "additionalProperties"}
        }
        properties = {
            key: _normalize_schema(schema["properties"][key])
            for key in sorted(schema["properties"])
        }
        return {
            **_canonical_mapping(meta),
            "properties": properties,
            "required": sorted(properties),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }
    if schema.get("type") == "array":
        meta = {key: value for key, value in schema.items() if key != "items"}
        return {
            **_canonical_mapping(meta),
            "items": _normalize_schema(schema["items"]),
        }
    return _canonical_mapping(schema)


def _validate_schema(schema: Any, *, path: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    if not isinstance(schema, Mapping):
        _invalid("严格输出 schema 节点必须是对象", path=path)
    schema_type = schema.get("type")
    if schema_type == "object":
        properties = schema.get("properties")
        required = schema.get("required")
        if not isinstance(properties, Mapping):
            _invalid("严格对象必须声明 properties", path=path)
        if schema.get("additionalProperties") is not False:
            _invalid("严格对象必须关闭 additionalProperties", path=path)
        if (
            not isinstance(required, list)
            or any(not isinstance(item, str) for item in required)
            or len(required) != len(set(required))
            or set(required) != set(properties)
        ):
            _invalid("严格对象的所有固定字段都必须 required", path=path)
        for key, child in properties.items():
            if not isinstance(key, str) or not key:
                _invalid("严格对象字段名不能为空", path=path)
            _validate_schema(child, path=f"{path}.{key}")
    elif schema_type == "array":
        if "items" not in schema:
            _invalid("严格数组必须声明 items", path=path)
        _validate_schema(schema["items"], path=f"{path}[]")
    elif schema_type is None:
        _invalid("严格输出 schema 节点必须声明 type", path=path)
    return schema


def _merge_schema(
    left: Mapping[str, Any],
    right: Mapping[str, Any],
    *,
    path: str,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if left.get("type") != right.get("type"):
        _conflict(path)
    schema_type = left.get("type")
    if schema_type == "object":
        left_meta = {
            key: value
            for key, value in left.items()
            if key not in {"properties", "required", "additionalProperties"}
        }
        right_meta = {
            key: value
            for key, value in right.items()
            if key not in {"properties", "required", "additionalProperties"}
        }
        if canonical_json(left_meta) != canonical_json(right_meta):
            _conflict(path)
        left_properties = left["properties"]
        right_properties = right["properties"]
        merged_properties: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for key in sorted(set(left_properties) | set(right_properties)):
            if key in left_properties and key in right_properties:
                merged_properties[key] = _merge_schema(
                    left_properties[key],
                    right_properties[key],
                    path=f"{path}.{key}",
                )
            elif key in left_properties:
                merged_properties[key] = _normalize_schema(left_properties[key])
            else:
                merged_properties[key] = _normalize_schema(right_properties[key])
        return {
            **_canonical_mapping(left_meta),
            "properties": merged_properties,
            "required": sorted(merged_properties),
            "additionalProperties": False,
        }
    if schema_type == "array":
        left_meta = {key: value for key, value in left.items() if key != "items"}
        right_meta = {key: value for key, value in right.items() if key != "items"}
        if canonical_json(left_meta) != canonical_json(right_meta):
            _conflict(path)
        return {
            **_canonical_mapping(left_meta),
            "items": _merge_schema(left["items"], right["items"], path=f"{path}[]"),
        }
    if canonical_json(left) != canonical_json(right):
        _conflict(path)
    return _canonical_mapping(left)


def merge_strict_prompt_contracts(
    contracts: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    name: str,
    description: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if (
        not isinstance(contracts, Sequence)
        or isinstance(contracts, (str, bytes))
        or not contracts
    ):
        _invalid("至少需要一个严格输出合同")
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
        _invalid("合并合同必须有稳定名称")
    if not isinstance(description, str):
        _invalid("合并合同描述必须是文本")

    schemas: list[Mapping[str, Any]] = []
    for index, contract in enumerate(contracts):
        if not isinstance(contract, Mapping):
            _invalid("严格输出合同必须是对象", path=f"$[{index}]")
        if contract.get("mode") != "json_schema" or contract.get("strict") is not True:
            _invalid("只能合并 mode=json_schema 且 strict=true 的合同", path=f"$[{index}]")
        schema = _validate_schema(contract.get("schema"), path="$")
        if schema.get("type") != "object":
            _invalid("严格输出合同根节点必须是对象", path="$")
        schemas.append(schema)

    ordered = sorted(schemas, key=canonical_json)
    merged = _normalize_schema(ordered[0])
    for schema in ordered[1:]:
        merged = _merge_schema(merged, schema, path="$")
    return {
        "mode": "json_schema",
        "name": name.strip(),
        "description": description,
        "strict": True,
        "schema": merged,
    }


def prompt_contract_hash(contract: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(canonical_json(contract).encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()

"""存量实验 API 基础服务。

提示词/字段便捷候选保持模型与路由不变；stock_lab 提供独立完整草稿、
组件、样本和确认后参考答案修订。只开放已登记未退役类目。
不改增量绑定、共享配置或正式下游；写入串行、幂等和审计。
旧 cancel 仅取消自身回归；新 control 逐项校验原生存量身份。
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence
import uuid

from sqlalchemy import and_, func, select, text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session, selectinload

from .audit import append_audit_event
from .models import (
    Asset,
    AuditEvent,
    BaselineRegressionItem,
    BaselineRegressionRun,
    BaselineSet,
    BaselineSetItem,
    BaselineTruthRevision,
    PromptVersion,
    WorkflowNodeAsset,
    WorkflowNodeAssetBinding,
    WorkflowVersion,
)

def allowed_categories(db: Session) -> frozenset[str]:
    from .models import EvaluationCategoryProfile
    return frozenset(db.scalars(select(EvaluationCategoryProfile.category_key).where(
        EvaluationCategoryProfile.status != "retired")))
LAB_ACTOR = "prompt-lab:codex"
LAB_VERSION_PREFIX = "lab-"
MAX_PROMPT_CHARS = 30_000
MAX_NOTE_CHARS = 2_000
MAX_ACTIVE_RUNS = 1
DEFAULT_DAILY_ITEM_CAP = 12_000
MAX_ITEMS_PAGE = 500
#: 实验室只跑真实模型；测试里换成 simulated 走夹具执行器。
MODEL_EXECUTION_MODE = "real"
_PLACEHOLDER = re.compile(r"\{\{[^{}]+\}\}")


class PromptLabError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str, *, status_code: int = 422) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code


LAB_LOCK_NAME = "label-system:prompt-lab"
LAB_LOCK_WAIT_SECONDS = 30
_LOCK_ENGINES: dict[str, Any] = {}


def _lock_engine(business_engine: Any) -> Any:
    """命名锁专用连接通道（ls-review-r24 F1）。

    锁连接若从业务连接池借，持锁者做事务还要再借一条，等锁的请求也各占一条等着，
    池一小（合法配置 pool_size=1/2、无溢出）就互相卡死。这里用同一个库地址另建不入池的
    NullPool 引擎：等锁只占自己的物理连接，关闭即断开，MySQL 随之释放会话级命名锁。
    """
    key = business_engine.url.render_as_string(hide_password=False)
    engine = _LOCK_ENGINES.get(key)
    if engine is None:
        from sqlalchemy import create_engine
        from sqlalchemy.pool import NullPool

        engine = create_engine(business_engine.url, poolclass=NullPool, connect_args={"connect_timeout": 10})
        _LOCK_ENGINES[key] = engine
    return engine


class LabWriteLock:
    """实验室写操作（建候选、开回归、取消）的全局串行锁。

    ls-review-r23 R1/S1：并发请求各自读到"没有在跑、额度够"，就能越过一轮上限与日额度；
    同一候选首次并发创建会撞唯一键成 500。所以必须在本请求第一次读库之前拿锁、提交之后才放：
    MySQL 用独立连接上的命名锁（会话连接提交后会还回连接池，锁不能挂在它上面），
    锁前没有读过库也就没有旧的可重复读视图；SQLite 在本会话 BEGIN IMMEDIATE。
    """

    def __init__(self, db: Session) -> None:
        self._db = db
        self._conn = None

    def __enter__(self) -> "LabWriteLock":
        if self._db.in_transaction():
            raise RuntimeError("prompt lab lock must be taken before the first read of this session")
        engine = self._db.get_bind()
        if engine.dialect.name == "mysql":
            conn = _lock_engine(engine).connect()
            try:
                got = conn.execute(
                    text("SELECT GET_LOCK(:name, :wait)"), {"name": LAB_LOCK_NAME, "wait": LAB_LOCK_WAIT_SECONDS}
                ).scalar()
            except BaseException:
                # 服务端可能已拿到锁而客户端没读到结果（ls-review-r24 F3）：作废并物理断开，锁随会话释放。
                conn.invalidate()
                conn.close()
                raise
            if got != 1:
                conn.close()
                raise PromptLabError("lab_busy", "实验室正在处理另一个写请求，请稍后重试", status_code=409)
            self._conn = conn
        else:
            from .database_capabilities import DatabaseCapabilities

            DatabaseCapabilities.for_dialect(engine.dialect.name).reserve_claim_transaction(self._db)
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc: Any) -> None:
        if self._conn is None:
            return
        conn, self._conn = self._conn, None
        try:
            conn.execute(text("SELECT RELEASE_LOCK(:name)"), {"name": LAB_LOCK_NAME})
        finally:
            conn.close()  # NullPool：物理断开，即使 RELEASE_LOCK 失败锁也随会话释放


def daily_item_cap() -> int:
    raw = os.getenv("PROMPT_LAB_DAILY_ITEM_CAP", "").strip()
    if raw.isdigit() and 0 < int(raw) <= 100_000:
        return int(raw)
    return DEFAULT_DAILY_ITEM_CAP


def _digest(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(
        json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str).encode()
    ).hexdigest()


def _json(value: str | None) -> Any:
    try:
        return json.loads(value or "{}")
    except json.JSONDecodeError:
        return None


def _iso(value: datetime | None) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if value.tzinfo is None:
        value = value.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return value.astimezone(timezone(timedelta(hours=8))).isoformat()


def _manifest(version: WorkflowVersion) -> dict[str, Any]:
    from .workflow_registry import persisted_workflow_manifest

    return persisted_workflow_manifest(version)


def _require_version(db: Session, version_id: int) -> tuple[WorkflowVersion, dict[str, Any]]:
    version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, version_id)
    if version is None or version.workflow_schema_version != "workflow-v3":
        raise PromptLabError("mechanism_not_found", "评测机制版本不存在", status_code=404)
    manifest = _manifest(version)
    if manifest.get("category_key") not in allowed_categories(db):
        raise PromptLabError("category_not_allowed", "这个评测机制不在实验室开放的类目内", status_code=403)
    return version, manifest


def _prompt_nodes(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[Mapping[str, Any]]:
    return [
        node
        for node in manifest.get("nodes") or []
        if isinstance(node, Mapping)
        and isinstance(node.get("config"), Mapping)
        and "prompt_version_id" in node["config"]
    ]


def _prompt_content(node: Mapping[str, Any]) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
    snapshot = node["config"].get("component_snapshot") or {}
    content = snapshot.get("content") if isinstance(snapshot, Mapping) else None
    if not isinstance(content, Mapping):
        raise PromptLabError("mechanism_prompt_snapshot_missing", "机制里的提示词节点缺少冻结内容", status_code=409)
    return content


def _comparable(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """比对"候选只差提示词"时去掉模型计费价目表：调价只影响预算，不改模型行为。

    .137 实测：价目表 v2→v3 之后重新冻结老机制，模型快照只有 pricing_contract 与随之变化的
    snapshot_hash 不同，原来会把所有老机制都判成"除提示词外不一致"。模型身份、参数、输出合同照样严格比对。
    """
    copy = deepcopy(dict(manifest))
    for node in copy.get("nodes") or []:
        snapshot = (node.get("config") or {}).get("model_snapshot") if isinstance(node, dict) else None
        if isinstance(snapshot, dict):
            snapshot.pop("snapshot_hash", None)
            runtime = snapshot.get("runtime_config")
            if isinstance(runtime, dict):
                runtime.pop("pricing_contract", None)
    return copy


def is_lab_version(version: WorkflowVersion) -> bool:
    return version.created_by == LAB_ACTOR and version.version.startswith((LAB_VERSION_PREFIX, "stock-"))


def _routes(manifest: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    return [
        {"branch": edge.get("branch"), "to": edge.get("to"), "condition": edge.get("condition")}
        for edge in manifest.get("edges") or []
        if isinstance(edge, Mapping) and edge.get("branch")
    ]


def describe_mechanism(db: Session, version_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    version, manifest = _require_version(db, version_id)
    nodes = []
    for node in _prompt_nodes(manifest):
        cfg = node["config"]
        content = _prompt_content(node)
        system_prompt = str(content.get("system_prompt") or "")
        user_prompt = str(content.get("user_prompt") or "")
        nodes.append({
            "node_id": node.get("node_instance_id"),
            "display_name": node.get("display_name"),
            "prompt_version_id": cfg.get("prompt_version_id"),
            "system_prompt": system_prompt,
            "user_prompt": user_prompt,
            "placeholders": sorted(set(_PLACEHOLDER.findall(system_prompt + "\n" + user_prompt))),
            "output_contract_key": cfg.get("output_contract_key"),
            "output_contract": cfg.get("output_contract_snapshot") or cfg.get("output_contract"),
            "model": {key: (cfg.get("model_snapshot") or {}).get(key) for key in ("provider", "model_id", "name")}
            if isinstance(cfg.get("model_snapshot"), Mapping) else None,
            "temperature": cfg.get("temperature"),
        })
    return {
        "id": version.id,
        "version": version.version,
        "canonical_hash": version.canonical_hash,
        "category_key": manifest.get("category_key"),
        "status": version.status,
        "created_by": version.created_by,
        "created_at": _iso(version.created_at),
        "is_lab_candidate": is_lab_version(version),
        "routes": _routes(manifest),
        "prompt_nodes": nodes,
    }


MAX_MECHANISMS_SCANNED = 500


def list_mechanisms_page(
    db: Session,
    *,
    limit: int = 30,
    before_id: int | None = None,
    scan_cap: int = MAX_MECHANISMS_SCANNED,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """有界分页列出实验室可用的评测机制版本（按 id 倒序）。

    Astra 全仓复审 F1：原实现不带 LIMIT 把全部 workflow-v3 版本连同大正文载入再在 Python 里过滤。
    现在先用流程定义的 allowed_categories_json 在 SQL 里缩小范围，按 id 分页只取 id，
    每条再单独载入核对冻结类目并立即移出会话，内存上界按页大小而不是机制总量。

    复审 N1：一个流程定义可能同时允许多个类目，冻结类目不在开放范围的版本会被跳过；一次调用最多检查
    ``scan_cap`` 个版本（每页取 min(limit, 剩余额度)，上限在页内检查），扫满还没凑够 ``limit`` 条时
    返回 ``scan_capped=True``，不再静默返回空列表或残缺列表。还有更早的版本时给出 ``next_before_id``，
    调用方带上 ``before_id`` 接着翻。
    """
    from .models import WorkflowDefinition

    categories = allowed_categories(db)
    definition_ids = [
        definition_id
        for definition_id, allowed in db.execute(
            select(WorkflowDefinition.id, WorkflowDefinition.allowed_categories_json)
        )
        if isinstance(parsed := _json(allowed), list) and set(parsed) & categories
    ]
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    page_info: dict[str, Any] = {"items": out, "next_before_id": None, "scan_capped": False, "scanned": 0}
    if not definition_ids:
        return page_info
    base = select(WorkflowVersion.id).where(
        WorkflowVersion.workflow_schema_version == "workflow-v3",
        WorkflowVersion.workflow_definition_id.in_(definition_ids),
    )
    last_id = before_id
    scanned = 0
    exhausted = False
    while len(out) < limit and scanned < scan_cap:
        query = base if last_id is None else base.where(WorkflowVersion.id < last_id)
        page = db.scalars(query.order_by(WorkflowVersion.id.desc()).limit(min(limit, scan_cap - scanned))).all()
        if not page:
            exhausted = True
            break
        for version_id in page:
            last_id = version_id
            scanned += 1
            version = db.get(WorkflowVersion, version_id)
            category = _manifest(version).get("category_key")
            if category in categories:
                out.append({
                    "id": version.id,
                    "version": version.version,
                    "canonical_hash": version.canonical_hash,
                    "category_key": category,
                    "status": version.status,
                    "created_by": version.created_by,
                    "created_at": _iso(version.created_at),
                    "is_lab_candidate": is_lab_version(version),
                })
            db.expunge(version)
            if len(out) >= limit:
                break
    if not exhausted and last_id is not None:
        # 只查一个 id 判断后面还有没有，避免"刚好扫完"时误报不完整。
        exhausted = db.scalar(base.where(WorkflowVersion.id < last_id).limit(1)) is None
    page_info["scanned"] = scanned
    if not exhausted:
        page_info["next_before_id"] = last_id
        page_info["scan_capped"] = len(out) < limit
    return page_info


def list_mechanisms(db: Session, *, limit: int = 30) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """兼容旧调用：只要第一页的条目。需要判断是否扫满上限、继续翻页时用 list_mechanisms_page。"""
    return list_mechanisms_page(db, limit=limit)["items"]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PromptEdit:
    system_prompt: str | None = None
    user_prompt: str | None = None


def _checked_text(value: str, *, field: str, node_id: str, allowed: set[str], allow_empty: bool = False) -> str:
    if not isinstance(value, str) or (not value.strip() and not allow_empty):
        raise PromptLabError("prompt_text_empty", f"{node_id}.{field} 不能为空")
    if len(value) > MAX_PROMPT_CHARS:
        raise PromptLabError("prompt_text_too_long", f"{node_id}.{field} 超过 {MAX_PROMPT_CHARS} 字")
    unknown = sorted(set(_PLACEHOLDER.findall(value)) - allowed)
    if unknown:
        raise PromptLabError(
            "prompt_placeholder_not_allowed",
            f"{node_id}.{field} 出现源提示词里没有的占位符：" + "、".join(unknown),
        )
    return value


def create_candidate(
    db: Session,
    *,
    source_version_id: int,
    expected_source_hash: str,
    edits: Mapping[str, PromptEdit],
    output_schemas: Mapping[str, dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    note: str = "",
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """在源机制上修改提示词和输出字段定义，冻结成一个新的评测机制版本（同样的输入得到同一个版本）。"""

    from .workflow_working_copies import WorkflowWorkingCopyError, create_immutable_evaluation_mechanism_version

    source, before = _require_version(db, source_version_id)
    if source.canonical_hash != expected_source_hash:
        raise PromptLabError("source_hash_mismatch", "源机制的 canonical_hash 与请求不一致，请重新读取机制", status_code=409)
    if len(note) > MAX_NOTE_CHARS:
        raise PromptLabError("note_too_long", f"说明不能超过 {MAX_NOTE_CHARS} 字")
