_IDENTIFIER = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$")
_KEY = re.compile(r"^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$")
_HASH = re.compile(r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
_STATUSES = {"draft", "active", "retired"}
_REQUIRED_MAPPINGS = {"content_id", "source_version", "category_key", "occurred_at"}
_FORBIDDEN_LOCATOR_PARTS = ("://", "password=", "token=", "secret=")


class ReadOnlySourceError(ValueError):
    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourceSafetyEvidence:
    read_only: bool
    verifier: str
    schema_fingerprint: str = ""
    detail: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourceCursor:
    values: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourceRow:
    content_id: str
    source_version: str
    category_key: str
    occurred_at: datetime
    asset_id: int | None = None
    event_type: str = "content.created"
    source_payload: Mapping[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    change_sequence: int | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SourcePage:
    rows: tuple[SourceRow, ...]
    next_cursor: SourceCursor | None
    schema_fingerprint: str


class ReadOnlySourceAdapter(Protocol):
    def verify_read_only(self) -> SourceSafetyEvidence: ...

    def fetch_page(
        self, *, cursor: SourceCursor | None, limit: int
    ) -> SourcePage: ...


class FixtureReadOnlySourceAdapter:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        read_only: bool,
        rows: Sequence[SourceRow],
        schema_fingerprint: str = "f" * 64,
    ) -> None:
        self._read_only = read_only
        self._rows = tuple(rows)
        self._schema_fingerprint = schema_fingerprint
        self.verify_count = 0
        self.fetch_count = 0
        self.last_limit: int | None = None

    def verify_read_only(self) -> SourceSafetyEvidence:
        self.verify_count += 1
        return SourceSafetyEvidence(
            read_only=self._read_only,
            verifier="fixture-explicit-read-only",
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
        )

    def fetch_page(
        self, *, cursor: SourceCursor | None, limit: int
    ) -> SourcePage:
        self.fetch_count += 1
        self.last_limit = limit
        rows = self._rows[:limit]
        next_cursor = (
            SourceCursor(
                {
                    "content_id": rows[-1].content_id,
                    "source_version": rows[-1].source_version,
                }
            )
            if rows
            else cursor
        )
        return SourcePage(
            rows=rows,
            next_cursor=next_cursor,
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
        )


class SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter:
    """Narrow SQL page adapter; it intentionally exposes no generic query API."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        connection_factory: Callable[[], ContextManager[Connection]],
        table_name: str,
        field_mappings: Mapping[str, str],
        schema_fingerprint: str,
        cursor_definition: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self._cursor_definition = dict(cursor_definition or {})
        self._connection_factory = connection_factory
        self._table_name = _safe_identifier(table_name)
        self._field_mappings = {
            logical: _safe_identifier(column)
            for logical, column in field_mappings.items()
        }
        if not _REQUIRED_MAPPINGS <= set(self._field_mappings):
            raise ReadOnlySourceError(
                "SOURCE_MAPPING_INVALID", "SQL 来源字段映射不完整"
            )
        self._schema_fingerprint = _validate_hash(schema_fingerprint)

    def _verify_connection_read_only(
        self, connection: Connection
    ) -> SourceSafetyEvidence:
        dialect = connection.dialect.name
        if dialect == "sqlite":
            value = connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA query_only").scalar_one()
            read_only = bool(value)
            verifier = "sqlite:pragma-query-only"
        elif dialect == "postgresql":
            value = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SHOW transaction_read_only"
            ).scalar_one()
            read_only = str(value).strip().lower() in {"on", "true", "1"}
            verifier = "postgresql:transaction-read-only"
        elif dialect in {"mysql", "mariadb"}:
            value = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT @@transaction_read_only"
            ).scalar_one()
            read_only = bool(value)
            verifier = f"{dialect}:transaction-read-only"
        else:
            return SourceSafetyEvidence(
                read_only=False,
                verifier=f"unsupported:{dialect}",
                schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
            )
        return SourceSafetyEvidence(
            read_only=read_only,
            verifier=verifier,
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
        )

    def verify_read_only(self) -> SourceSafetyEvidence:
        with self._connection_factory() as connection:
            return self._verify_connection_read_only(connection)

    def fetch_page(
        self, *, cursor: SourceCursor | None, limit: int
    ) -> SourcePage:
        _validate_limit(limit)
        definition = self._cursor_definition
        mode = definition.get("mode", "snapshot")
        if mode not in {"snapshot", "change"}:
            raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CURSOR_INVALID", "未知来源游标模式")
        fields = [str(definition.get("field", "change_cursor"))] if mode == "change" else ["content_id", "source_version"]
        if any(name not in self._field_mappings for name in fields):
            raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CURSOR_INVALID", "变化游标必须映射全局唯一单调变化序号")
        values = dict(cursor.values) if cursor else {}
        if values and any(name not in values for name in fields):
            raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CURSOR_INVALID", "游标与合同不一致")
        params: dict[str, Any] = {"limit": limit}
        with self._connection_factory() as connection:
            evidence = self._verify_connection_read_only(connection)
            if not evidence.read_only:
                raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_NOT_READ_ONLY", "分页读取连接无法证明为只读")
            quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
            columns = [quote(self._field_mappings[name]) for name in fields]
            aliases = sorted(self._field_mappings)
            select_list = ", ".join(f"{quote(self._field_mappings[name])} AS {quote(name)}" for name in aliases)
            where = ""
            if values:
                params.update({f"cursor_{i}": values[name] for i, name in enumerate(fields)})
                terms = []
                for i, column in enumerate(columns):
                    terms.append("(" + " AND ".join([f"{columns[j]} = :cursor_{j}" for j in range(i)] + [f"{column} > :cursor_{i}"]) + ")")
                where = " WHERE " + " OR ".join(terms)
            rows = connection.execute(text(f"SELECT {select_list} FROM {quote(self._table_name)}{where} ORDER BY {', '.join(columns)} LIMIT :limit"), params).mappings().all()
        next_cursor = SourceCursor({name: rows[-1][name] for name in fields}) if rows else cursor
        # Raw rows reach the transactional ingestion boundary so normalization failures
        # can be quarantined without discarding the other records in this page.
        mapped_rows = [dict(row, change_sequence=row[fields[0]]) if mode == "change" else dict(row) for row in rows]
        normalized = tuple(mapped_rows) if definition.get("failure_policy") == "isolate" else tuple(_source_row_from_mapping(row) for row in mapped_rows)
        return SourcePage(rows=normalized, next_cursor=next_cursor, schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint)


def _safe_identifier(value: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip()
    if not _IDENTIFIER.fullmatch(normalized):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_IDENTIFIER_INVALID", "来源表名和列名必须是已校验标识符"
        )
    return normalized


def _validate_hash(value: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip().lower()
    if not _HASH.fullmatch(normalized):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_SCHEMA_FINGERPRINT_INVALID", "schema fingerprint 必须是 SHA-256"
        )
    return normalized


def _required_text(value: str, *, label: str) -> str:
    normalized = value.strip()
    if not normalized:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CONTRACT_INVALID", f"{label}不能为空")
    return normalized


def _validate_limit(limit: int) -> None:
    if isinstance(limit, bool) or not isinstance(limit, int) or limit < 1 or limit > 500:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_PAGE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED", "来源单次读取上限为 500 条"
        )


def _source_row_from_mapping(row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> SourceRow:
    occurred_at = row["occurred_at"]
    if isinstance(occurred_at, str):
        occurred_at = datetime.fromisoformat(occurred_at.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
    asset_id = row.get("asset_id")
    return SourceRow(
        content_id=str(row["content_id"]),
        source_version=str(row["source_version"]),
        category_key=str(row["category_key"]),
        occurred_at=occurred_at,
        asset_id=int(asset_id) if asset_id is not None else None,
        event_type=str(row.get("event_type") or "content.created"),
        source_payload=dict(row.get("source_payload") or {}),
        change_sequence=row.get("change_sequence"),
    )


def create_upstream_source_contract(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract_key: str,
    adapter_key: str,
    source_system: str,
    category_key: str,
    connection_locator: str,
    secret_reference: str,
    field_mappings: Mapping[str, str],
    cursor_definition: Mapping[str, Any],
    page_size: int,
    read_only: bool,
    poll_interval_seconds: int | None = None,
    schema_fingerprint: str,
    owner: str,
    status: str,
    created_by: str,
) -> UpstreamSourceContract:
    normalized_key = _required_text(contract_key, label="contract_key")
    if not _KEY.fullmatch(normalized_key):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CONTRACT_INVALID", "contract_key 格式无效")
    normalized_adapter = _required_text(adapter_key, label="adapter_key")
    normalized_source = _required_text(source_system, label="source_system")
    normalized_category = _required_text(category_key, label="category_key")
    normalized_locator = _required_text(connection_locator, label="connection_locator")
    normalized_secret_ref = _required_text(secret_reference, label="secret_reference")
    if any(part in normalized_locator.lower() for part in _FORBIDDEN_LOCATOR_PARTS):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_CONTRACT_SECRET_EXPOSED", "connection_locator 只能保存逻辑标识"
        )
    if any(part in normalized_secret_ref.lower() for part in _FORBIDDEN_LOCATOR_PARTS):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_CONTRACT_SECRET_EXPOSED", "secret_reference 只能保存引用键"
        )
    if not read_only:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_NOT_READ_ONLY", "来源合同必须声明为只读"
        )
    _validate_limit(page_size)
    normalized_status = status.strip().lower()
    if normalized_status not in _STATUSES:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CONTRACT_INVALID", "来源合同状态无效")
    mappings = {str(key): str(value).strip() for key, value in field_mappings.items()}
    if not _REQUIRED_MAPPINGS <= set(mappings) or any(not value for value in mappings.values()):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_MAPPING_INVALID", "来源字段映射必须包含内容、版本、类目和时间"
        )
    mode = cursor_definition.get("mode", "snapshot")
    if mode not in {"snapshot", "change"} or cursor_definition.get("failure_policy", "atomic") not in {"atomic", "isolate"}:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CURSOR_INVALID", "来源游标模式或失败策略无效")
    if mode == "change" and str(cursor_definition.get("field", "change_cursor")) not in mappings:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CURSOR_INVALID", "变化模式必须映射全局唯一单调序号")
    fingerprint = _validate_hash(schema_fingerprint)
    definition = {
        "contract_key": normalized_key,
        "adapter_key": normalized_adapter,
        "source_system": normalized_source,
        "category_key": normalized_category,
        "connection_locator": normalized_locator,
        "secret_reference": normalized_secret_ref,
        "field_mappings": dict(sorted(mappings.items())),
        "cursor_definition": dict(cursor_definition),
        "page_size": page_size,
        "read_only": True,
        "schema_fingerprint": fingerprint,
        "owner": _required_text(owner, label="owner"),
        "status": normalized_status,
    }
    contract_hash = hashlib.sha256(
        canonical_json(definition).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    existing = db.scalar(
        select(UpstreamSourceContract).where(
            UpstreamSourceContract.contract_key == normalized_key,
            UpstreamSourceContract.contract_hash == contract_hash,
        )
    )
    if existing is not None:
        return existing
    latest_version = db.scalar(
        select(func.max(UpstreamSourceContract.version)).where(
            UpstreamSourceContract.contract_key == normalized_key
        )
    )
    contract = UpstreamSourceContract(
        contract_key=normalized_key,
        version=int(latest_version or 0) + 1,
        adapter_key=normalized_adapter,
        source_system=normalized_source,
        category_key=normalized_category,
        connection_locator=normalized_locator,
        secret_reference=normalized_secret_ref,
        field_mappings_json=canonical_json(dict(sorted(mappings.items()))),
        cursor_definition_json=canonical_json(dict(cursor_definition)),
        page_size=page_size,
        read_only=True,
        poll_interval_seconds=poll_interval_seconds,
        schema_fingerprint=fingerprint,
        owner=definition["owner"],
        status=normalized_status,
        contract_hash=contract_hash,
        created_by=_required_text(created_by, label="created_by"),
    )
    db.add(contract)
    db.flush()
    return contract


def _cursor_payload(cursor: SourceCursor | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return dict(cursor.values) if cursor is not None else {}


def _row_payload(row: SourceRow) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if isinstance(row, Mapping):
        return {key: platform_isoformat(value) if isinstance(value, datetime) else value
                for key, value in row.items()}
    occurred_at = row.occurred_at
    if occurred_at.tzinfo is None:
        occurred_at = occurred_at.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return {
        "content_id": row.content_id,
        "source_version": row.source_version,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
        "occurred_at": occurred_at.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
        "asset_id": row.asset_id,
        "event_type": row.event_type,
        "source_payload": dict(row.source_payload),
        "change_sequence": row.change_sequence,
    }


def ingest_source_row(db: Session, *, contract: UpstreamSourceContract, row: SourceRow, actor: str):
    if isinstance(row, Mapping):
        row = _source_row_from_mapping(row)
    change_order = None
    if json.loads(contract.cursor_definition_json).get("mode", "snapshot") == "change":
        if isinstance(row.change_sequence, bool) or not isinstance(row.change_sequence, int):
            raise ReadOnlySourceError("SOURCE_CHANGE_SEQUENCE_INVALID", "变化来源必须提供整数全局单调序号")
        change_order = (contract.contract_key, row.change_sequence)
    if row.category_key != contract.category_key:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_CATEGORY_MISMATCH", "来源行类目与合同不一致"
        )
    asset: Asset | None = None
    asset_version_id: int | None = None
    if row.asset_id is not None:
        asset = db.get(Asset, row.asset_id)
        if asset is None or asset.category_key != row.category_key:
            raise ReadOnlySourceError(
                "SOURCE_ASSET_INVALID", "来源行绑定素材不存在或类目不一致"
            )
        asset_version, _asset_duplicate = record_asset_version(
            db,
            source_system=contract.source_system,
            source_content_id=row.content_id,
            source_version=row.source_version,
            asset=asset,
            occurred_at=row.occurred_at,
        )
        asset_version_id = asset_version.id
    event_id = "readonly-source:" + hashlib.sha256(
        (
            f"{contract.source_system}:{row.content_id}:"
            f"{row.source_version}:{row.event_type}"
        ).encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        **dict(row.source_payload),
        "content_id": row.content_id,
        "content_version": row.source_version,
        "category_key": row.category_key,
    }
    if asset is not None:
        payload["asset_id"] = asset.id
    event, record, duplicate = ingest_content_event(
        db,
        event_id=event_id,
        schema_version="content-ingress-v1",
        event_type=row.event_type,
        source_system=contract.source_system,
        occurred_at=row.occurred_at,
        payload=payload,
        received_by=actor,
        source_change_order=change_order,
    )
    if asset_version_id is not None and event.status != "stale":
        record.current_asset_version_id = asset_version_id
    dispatch = dispatch_content_event(
        db,
        event=event,
        record=record,
        duplicate=duplicate,
        actor=actor,
    )
    return duplicate, dispatch


def poll_upstream_source(
    db: Session,
    *,
    contract: UpstreamSourceContract,
    adapter: ReadOnlySourceAdapter,
    limit: int,
    actor: str,
    cursor: SourceCursor | None = None,
) -> UpstreamReadRun:
    _validate_limit(limit)
    run = UpstreamReadRun(
        source_contract_id=contract.id,
        source_contract_hash=contract.contract_hash,
        category_key=contract.category_key,
        requested_cursor_json=canonical_json(_cursor_payload(cursor)),
        requested_limit=limit,
        status="running",
        actor=_required_text(actor, label="actor"),
    )
    db.add(run)
    db.flush()
    if contract.status != "active" or not contract.read_only:
        run.status = "blocked"
        run.error_code = "SOURCE_CONTRACT_INACTIVE"
        run.error_message = "来源合同未启用或未声明只读"
        return run

    try:
        evidence = adapter.verify_read_only()
        if not evidence.read_only:
            run.status = "blocked"
            run.error_code = "SOURCE_NOT_READ_ONLY"
            run.error_message = "连接时无法证明来源为只读"
            return run
        if (
            evidence.schema_fingerprint
            and evidence.schema_fingerprint != contract.schema_fingerprint
        ):
            run.status = "blocked"
            run.error_code = "SOURCE_SCHEMA_DRIFT"
            run.error_message = "只读证据中的 schema fingerprint 与合同不一致"
            run.schema_fingerprint = evidence.schema_fingerprint
            return run
        page = adapter.fetch_page(cursor=cursor, limit=limit)
        run.schema_fingerprint = page.schema_fingerprint
        run.next_cursor_json = canonical_json(_cursor_payload(page.next_cursor))
        run.page_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            canonical_json([_row_payload(row) for row in page.rows]).encode("utf-8")
        ).hexdigest()
        if page.schema_fingerprint != contract.schema_fingerprint:
            run.status = "blocked"
            run.error_code = "SOURCE_SCHEMA_DRIFT"
            run.error_message = "来源页面 schema fingerprint 与合同不一致"
            return run

        row_count = 0
        duplicate_count = 0
        package_count = 0
        awaiting_material_count = 0
        with db.begin_nested():
            for row in page.rows:
                try:
                    with db.begin_nested():
                        duplicate, dispatch = ingest_source_row(db, contract=contract, row=row, actor=actor)
                except Exception as exc:
                    if json.loads(contract.cursor_definition_json).get("failure_policy", "atomic") != "isolate":
                        raise
                    from .source_recovery import quarantine_source_row
                    quarantine_source_row(db, contract=contract, run=run, payload=_row_payload(row), error=exc)
                    continue
                row_count += 1
                duplicate_count += int(duplicate)
                package_count += int(dispatch.package_created)
                if dispatch.routing_status == "awaiting_material":
                    awaiting_material_count += 1
        run.row_count = row_count
        run.duplicate_count = duplicate_count
        run.package_count = package_count
        run.awaiting_material_count = awaiting_material_count
        run.status = "succeeded"
        return run
    except ReadOnlySourceError as exc:
        run.status = "blocked"
        run.error_code = exc.code
        run.error_message = str(exc)
        return run
    except Exception as exc:
        run.status = "failed"
        run.error_code = "SOURCE_READ_FAILED"
        run.error_message = type(exc).__name__
        return run


def source_contract_payload(contract: UpstreamSourceContract) -> dict[str, Any]:
    import json

    return {
        "id": contract.id,
        "contract_key": contract.contract_key,
        "version": contract.version,
        "adapter_key": contract.adapter_key,
        "source_system": contract.source_system,
        "category_key": contract.category_key,
        "connection_locator": contract.connection_locator,
        "secret_reference": contract.secret_reference,
        "secret_status": "unresolved",
        "field_mappings": json.loads(contract.field_mappings_json),
        "cursor_definition": json.loads(contract.cursor_definition_json),
        "source_mode": json.loads(contract.cursor_definition_json).get("mode", "snapshot"),
        "continuous_changes": json.loads(contract.cursor_definition_json).get("mode", "snapshot") == "change",
        "page_size": contract.page_size,
        "read_only": contract.read_only,
        "schema_fingerprint": contract.schema_fingerprint,
        "owner": contract.owner,
        "status": contract.status,
        "contract_hash": contract.contract_hash,
        "created_by": contract.created_by,
        "created_at": contract.created_at,
    }


def source_run_payload(run: UpstreamReadRun) -> dict[str, Any]:
    import json

    return {
        "id": run.id,
        "source_contract_id": run.source_contract_id,
        "source_contract_hash": run.source_contract_hash,
        "category_key": run.category_key,
        "requested_cursor": json.loads(run.requested_cursor_json),
        "next_cursor": json.loads(run.next_cursor_json),
        "requested_limit": run.requested_limit,
        "status": run.status,
        "schema_fingerprint": run.schema_fingerprint,
        "page_hash": run.page_hash,
        "row_count": run.row_count,
        "package_count": run.package_count,
        "duplicate_count": run.duplicate_count,
        "awaiting_material_count": run.awaiting_material_count,
        "error_code": run.error_code,
        "error_message": run.error_message,
        "attempt_count": run.attempt_count,
        "retry_after": run.retry_after,
        "actor": run.actor,
        "created_at": run.created_at,
        "updated_at": run.updated_at,
    }


def resolve_configured_readonly_source_adapter(
    contract: UpstreamSourceContract,
) -> ReadOnlySourceAdapter:
    """把上游合同登记行解析成可真正读库的只读适配器。

    这是上游取数链路此前唯一的断点：``/api/upstream-source-contracts/{id}/poll``
    直接返回 503 ``SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE``，而机制（合同、游标、分页、
    只读校验、读取运行记录、幂等落库）都已完整。

    与下游影子投影的解析器（``shadow_projection`` 内同名角色）是同一形状，共用
    ``external_datasources`` 这一层，因此安全口径一致：

    - **连接串与表名都不从合同里取。** 合同的 ``connection_locator`` 只是逻辑
      引用（``_required`` 禁止其含 ``://``），真实 DSN 与物理表名都配在部署侧。
      上游合同刻意没有 ``table_name`` 列——那属于部署细节，不该跟着合同行走。
    - **只读是数据库层强制的。** 连接工厂会把连接切成只读（SQLite 的
      ``PRAGMA query_only``、PostgreSQL 与 MySQL 的只读事务），所以适配器
      ``verify_read_only`` 的探测拿到的是真实结果，而不是被绕过的门禁。
    - **schema 指纹从活库实算。** 适配器的 ``schema_fingerprint`` 是构造参数
      原样回传，而 :func:`poll_upstream_source` 会拿它和合同登记值比对来检测漂移。
      若这里把合同登记值传进去，那道比对就变成自证同一、形同废除。
    """
    if not contract.read_only:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_CONTRACT_NOT_READ_ONLY",
            f"合同 {contract.contract_key} 未声明只读，拒绝解析取数适配器",
        )
    if contract.adapter_key != "sql":
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE",
            f"适配器类型 {contract.adapter_key} 尚无运行时实现；当前支持：sql",
        )

    try:
        field_mappings = json.loads(contract.field_mappings_json)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_MAPPING_INVALID", "字段映射不是合法 JSON"
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(field_mappings, Mapping):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_MAPPING_INVALID", "字段映射必须是对象"
        )

    try:
        table_name = resolve_locator_table(contract.connection_locator)
        live_fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
            contract.connection_locator,
            contract.secret_reference,
            table_name=table_name,
        )
        connection_factory = readonly_connection_factory(
            contract.connection_locator, contract.secret_reference
        )
    except ExternalDatasourceError as exc:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE",
            f"上游来源 {contract.contract_key} 连接不可用：{exc.message}",
        ) from exc

    return SqlReadOnlySourceAdapter(
        connection_factory=connection_factory,
        table_name=table_name,
        field_mappings={
            str(logical): str(column) for logical, column in field_mappings.items()
        },
        schema_fingerprint=live_fingerprint.lower(),
        cursor_definition=json.loads(contract.cursor_definition_json),
    )


class SqlConnectorPollAdapter:
    """Read one connector page without exposing a generic SQL surface."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        connection_factory: Callable[[], ContextManager[Connection]],
        table_name: str,
        field_mapping: Mapping[str, Any],
        schema_fingerprint: str,
        ordering: str,
    ) -> None:
        self._connection_factory = connection_factory
        self._table_name = _safe_identifier(table_name)
        self._schema_fingerprint = _validate_hash(schema_fingerprint)
        self._ordering = ordering
        if ordering not in {"ascending", "descending"}:
            raise ReadOnlySourceError(
                "SOURCE_CURSOR_INVALID",
                "连接器游标排序必须是 ascending 或 descending",
            )
        required = {"source_event_id", "source_cursor", "payload"}
        if not required <= set(field_mapping):
            raise ReadOnlySourceError(
                "SOURCE_MAPPING_INVALID",
                "连接器字段映射缺少事件编号、游标或业务内容",
            )
        payload_mapping = field_mapping.get("payload")
        if not isinstance(payload_mapping, Mapping) or not payload_mapping:
            raise ReadOnlySourceError(
                "SOURCE_MAPPING_INVALID",
                "连接器业务内容映射必须是非空对象",
            )
        self._event_column = _safe_identifier(
            str(field_mapping["source_event_id"])
        )
        self._cursor_column = _safe_identifier(
            str(field_mapping["source_cursor"])
        )
        occurred_column = field_mapping.get("source_occurred_at")
        self._occurred_column = (
            _safe_identifier(str(occurred_column))
            if occurred_column is not None
            else None
        )
        self._payload_columns = {
            str(logical): _safe_identifier(str(column))
            for logical, column in payload_mapping.items()
        }

    @staticmethod
    def _read_only_evidence(connection: Connection) -> PollSafetyEvidence:
        dialect = connection.dialect.name
        if dialect == "sqlite":
            value = connection.exec_driver_sql("PRAGMA query_only").scalar_one()
            read_only = bool(value)
            verifier = "sqlite:pragma-query-only"
        elif dialect == "postgresql":
            value = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SHOW transaction_read_only"
            ).scalar_one()
            read_only = str(value).strip().lower() in {"on", "true", "1"}
            verifier = "postgresql:transaction-read-only"
        elif dialect in {"mysql", "mariadb"}:
            value = connection.exec_driver_sql(
                "SELECT @@transaction_read_only"
            ).scalar_one()
            read_only = bool(value)
            verifier = f"{dialect}:transaction-read-only"
        else:
            read_only = False
            verifier = f"unsupported:{dialect}"
        return PollSafetyEvidence(
            read_only=read_only,
            schema_fingerprint="",
            verifier=verifier,
        )

    def verify_read_only(self) -> PollSafetyEvidence:
        with self._connection_factory() as connection:
            evidence = self._read_only_evidence(connection)
        return PollSafetyEvidence(
            read_only=evidence.read_only,
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
            verifier=evidence.verifier,
        )

    def fetch_page(self, *, after_cursor: str, limit: int) -> PollPage:
        _validate_limit(limit)
        with self._connection_factory() as connection:
            quote = connection.dialect.identifier_preparer.quote
            columns = [
                f"{quote(self._event_column)} AS connector_event_id",
                f"{quote(self._cursor_column)} AS connector_cursor",
            ]
            if self._occurred_column is not None:
                columns.append(
                    f"{quote(self._occurred_column)} AS connector_occurred_at"
                )
            payload_aliases: dict[str, str] = {}
            for index, (logical, column) in enumerate(
                sorted(self._payload_columns.items())
            ):
                alias = f"connector_payload_{index}"
                payload_aliases[logical] = alias
                columns.append(f"{quote(column)} AS {alias}")
            comparator = ">" if self._ordering == "ascending" else "<"
            direction = "ASC" if self._ordering == "ascending" else "DESC"
            where = (
                f" WHERE {quote(self._cursor_column)} {comparator} :after_cursor"
                if after_cursor
                else ""
            )
            statement = text(
                f"SELECT {', '.join(columns)} "
                f"FROM {quote(self._table_name)}{where} "
                f"ORDER BY {quote(self._cursor_column)} {direction} LIMIT :limit"
            )
            params: dict[str, Any] = {"limit": limit}
            if after_cursor:
                params["after_cursor"] = after_cursor
            rows = connection.execute(statement, params).mappings().all()

        records: list[PollRecord] = []
        for row in rows:
            occurred_at = row.get("connector_occurred_at")
            if isinstance(occurred_at, str):
                try:
                    occurred_at = datetime.fromisoformat(
                        occurred_at.replace("Z", "+00:00")
                    )
                except ValueError as exc:
                    raise ReadOnlySourceError(
                        "SOURCE_TIMESTAMP_INVALID",
                        "上游事件时间无法解析",
                    ) from exc
            records.append(
                PollRecord(
                    source_event_id=str(row["connector_event_id"]),
                    source_cursor=str(row["connector_cursor"]),
                    source_occurred_at=occurred_at,
                    payload={
                        logical: row[alias]
                        for logical, alias in payload_aliases.items()
                    },
                )
            )
        return PollPage(
            records=tuple(records),
            schema_fingerprint=self._schema_fingerprint,
        )


def resolve_configured_connector_poll_adapter(
    contract: ConnectorContractVersion,
) -> SqlConnectorPollAdapter:
    if contract.direction != "upstream" or contract.adapter_key != "sql-poll-v1":
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE",
            "该上游合同未配置只读轮询适配器",
        )
    try:
        mapping = json.loads(contract.field_mapping_json)
        cursor_contract = json.loads(contract.cursor_contract_json)
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_MAPPING_INVALID",
            "上游合同配置无法解析",
        ) from exc
    if not isinstance(mapping, Mapping) or not isinstance(
        cursor_contract,
        Mapping,
    ):
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_MAPPING_INVALID",
            "上游合同配置必须是对象",
        )
    try:
        secret_reference = resolve_locator_secret_reference(
            contract.logical_locator
        )
        table_name = resolve_locator_table(contract.logical_locator)
        live_fingerprint = compute_live_schema_fingerprint(
            contract.logical_locator,
            secret_reference,
            table_name=table_name,
        )
        connection_factory = readonly_connection_factory(
            contract.logical_locator,
            secret_reference,
        )
    except ExternalDatasourceError as exc:
        raise ReadOnlySourceError(
            "SOURCE_ADAPTER_UNAVAILABLE",
            "上游连接尚未配置或无法通过安全检查",
        ) from exc
    return SqlConnectorPollAdapter(
        connection_factory=connection_factory,
        table_name=table_name,
        field_mapping=mapping,
        schema_fingerprint=live_fingerprint,
        ordering=str(cursor_contract.get("ordering", "ascending")),
    )

===== FILE backend/app/recommendation_calibration.py =====
"""Opt-in, frozen 3DSU recommendation boundary learned from visual evidence.

This module performs no I/O. Material identities, titles, truth and baseline
membership are not inputs. A profile is bound to the exact A/B configurations;
its coefficients belong to the versioned server scoring rule, not the model.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

from copy import deepcopy
import hashlib
import json
import math
import re
import statistics
from typing import Any, Mapping, Sequence

from .level_scale import resolve_level_scale, score_to_level

SCHEMA = "stock-evidence-boundary-v1"
FEATURE_VERSION = "stock-visual-evidence-v1"
ENGINE_VERSION = "category-evaluation-evidence-boundary-v1"
PROFILE_KEY = "recommendation_calibration"
VOTE_AGGREGATIONS = ("mean", "min", "median")
DIMS = ("model_detail", "material_rendering", "lighting", "design_trend", "visual_composition")
POSITIVE = frozenset({"L1", "L2"})
_TEXT_FIELDS = ("a_cons", "a_defects", "a_scope_evidence", "a_render_evidence",
                "b_overall", "b_evidence", *("b_" + dim for dim in DIMS))
_CATEGORIES = ("track", "primary", "doc_primary", "scope", "quality", "rendered")
_FIXED = {"bias", "b_present", "base_score", "final_score"} | {
    f"rule/{dim}/{code}" for dim in DIMS for code in ("minor", "obvious_defect", "severe_defect")
}


def _canonical(value: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), allow_nan=False)


def digest(value: Any) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(_canonical(value).encode()).hexdigest()


def _number(value: Any, low: float, high: float) -> bool:
    return type(value) in (int, float) and math.isfinite(value) and low <= value <= high


def _mapping(value: Any) -> Mapping:
    return value if isinstance(value, Mapping) else {}


def _strings(value: Any):
    if isinstance(value, str):
        yield value[:4096]
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for item in value[:32]:
            if isinstance(item, str):
                yield item[:4096]


def evidence_features(precheck: Mapping, dimensions: Mapping, scoring: Mapping) -> tuple[dict[str, float], set[str]]:
    """Fixed structured signals plus common Chinese bigrams/trigrams.

    Callers cannot supply features through arbitrary payload keys. The training
    tool applies document-frequency limits before exporting its vocabulary.
    """
    a, b, n = precheck, dimensions, scoring
    fixed = {"bias": 1.0, "b_present": float(bool(b))}
    for name, value in (("base_score", b.get("aesthetic_score")), ("final_score", n.get("score"))):
        if _number(value, 0, 100):
            fixed[name] = value / 100
    for dim in DIMS:
        rules = _mapping(_mapping(b.get("dimensions")).get(dim)).get("hit_rules") or []
        for code in ("minor", "obvious_defect", "severe_defect"):
            fixed[f"rule/{dim}/{code}"] = float(any(
                (r.get("rule_id") if isinstance(r, Mapping) else r) == code for r in rules
            ))
    classification, media = _mapping(a.get("classification")), _mapping(a.get("media_form"))
    for name, value in (("track", n.get("track_key")), ("primary", classification.get("primary_category")),
        ("doc_primary", classification.get("doc_primary")), ("scope", classification.get("scope_status")),
        ("quality", _mapping(a.get("image_quality")).get("quality_severity")),
        ("rendered", _mapping(media.get("rendered_status")).get("status"))):
        if isinstance(value, str) and len(value) <= 64:
            fixed[f"category/{name}/{value}"] = 1.0
    production = _mapping(a.get("production_fields"))
    fields = {"a_cons": production.get("cons"), "a_defects": production.get("image_defects"),
        "a_scope_evidence": classification.get("evidence"),
        "a_render_evidence": _mapping(media.get("rendered_status")).get("evidence"),
        "b_overall": b.get("overall_note"), "b_evidence": b.get("aesthetic_evidence")}
    for dim in DIMS:
        fields["b_" + dim] = _mapping(_mapping(b.get("dimensions")).get(dim)).get("evaluation")
    text = set()
    for field, value in fields.items():
        for statement in _strings(value):
            for span in re.findall(r"[\u4e00-\u9fff]+", statement):
                for size in (2, 3):
                    text.update(f"text/{field}/{span[i:i+size]}" for i in range(len(span)-size+1))
    return {k: v for k, v in fixed.items() if v}, text


def _feature_kind(name: Any) -> str | None:
    if not isinstance(name, str):
        return None
    if name in _FIXED:
        return "fixed"
    parts = name.split("/")
    if len(parts) != 3:
        return None
    family, field, term = parts
    if family == "text" and field in _TEXT_FIELDS and re.fullmatch(r"[\u4e00-\u9fff]{2,3}", term):
        return "text"
    if family == "category" and field in _CATEGORIES and re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z_\u4e00-\u9fff]{1,64}", term):
        return "fixed"
    return None


def validate_profile(profile: Any) -> None:
    expected = {"schema_version", "feature_version", "threshold", "features", "bindings", "training"}
    optional = {"vote_aggregation"}
    if not isinstance(profile, dict) or not expected <= set(profile) <= expected | optional:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_profile_invalid")
    if "vote_aggregation" in profile and profile["vote_aggregation"] not in VOTE_AGGREGATIONS:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_vote_aggregation_invalid")
    if profile["schema_version"] != SCHEMA or profile["feature_version"] != FEATURE_VERSION:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_version_invalid")
    if not _number(profile["threshold"], .001, .999):
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_threshold_invalid")
    bindings = profile["bindings"]
    if (not isinstance(bindings, dict) or set(bindings) != {"prompt_a", "prompt_b", "grading_rules"}
            or any(not isinstance(v, str) or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", v) for v in bindings.values())):
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_bindings_invalid")
    training = profile["training"]
    if (not isinstance(training, dict) or set(training) != {"run_id", "rows", "source_sha256", "purpose", "minimum_document_frequency"}
            or type(training["run_id"]) is not int or training["run_id"] <= 0
            or type(training["rows"]) is not int or training["rows"] < 200
            or type(training["minimum_document_frequency"]) is not int
            or not 40 <= training["minimum_document_frequency"] <= training["rows"]
            or training["purpose"] != "golden_calibration"
            or not isinstance(training["source_sha256"], str)
            or not re.fullmatch(r"[0-9a-f]{64}", training["source_sha256"])):
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_training_invalid")
    entries = profile["features"]
    if not isinstance(entries, list) or not 1 <= len(entries) <= 5000:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_features_invalid")
    names = set()
    for entry in entries:
        if not isinstance(entry, list) or len(entry) != 3:
            raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_feature_invalid")
        name, weight, idf = entry
        kind = _feature_kind(name)
        if not kind or name in names or not _number(weight, -100, 100):
            raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_feature_invalid")
        if (kind == "text" and not _number(idf, 1, 20)) or (kind == "fixed" and idf is not None):
            raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_idf_invalid")
        names.add(name)
    if "bias" not in names or len(_canonical(profile).encode()) > 500_000:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_size_invalid")


def mechanism_bindings(manifest: Mapping) -> dict[str, str]:
    prompts = [node for node in manifest.get("nodes", []) if node.get("kind") == "prompt"]
    configs = {_mapping(node.get("config")).get("asset_key"): _mapping(node.get("config")) for node in prompts}
    if len(prompts) != 2 or set(configs) != {"prompt_a", "prompt_b"}:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_ab_required")
    for config in configs.values():
        model = _mapping(config.get("model_snapshot"))
        if (model.get("provider") != "local_qwen" or not model.get("snapshot_hash")
                or not _mapping(config.get("component_snapshot")).get("content_hash")):
            raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_frozen_qwen_required")
    result = {key: digest(config) for key, config in configs.items()}
    rules = {}
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        config = _mapping(node.get("config"))
        if node.get("kind") != "rule_module":
            continue
        key = config.get("module_type_key")
        raw = _mapping(config.get("component_snapshot")).get("content_json")
        if not isinstance(key, str) or key in rules or not isinstance(raw, str):
            raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_rules_invalid")
        content = json.loads(raw)
        if not isinstance(content, dict):
            raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_rules_invalid")
        if key == "score_level_matcher":
            content.pop(PROFILE_KEY, None)
        rules[key] = content
    if "score_level_matcher" not in rules:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_matcher_required")
    result["grading_rules"] = digest(rules)
    return result


def manifest_profile(manifest: Mapping) -> tuple[dict, dict] | None:
    profiles = []
    for node in manifest.get("nodes", []):
        config = _mapping(node.get("config"))
        if node.get("kind") != "rule_module" or config.get("module_type_key") != "score_level_matcher":
            continue
        raw = _mapping(config.get("component_snapshot")).get("content_json")
        if not isinstance(raw, str):
            continue
        try:
            matcher = json.loads(raw)
        except ValueError:
            continue  # The existing frozen rule validator diagnoses malformed JSON.
        if isinstance(matcher, dict) and PROFILE_KEY in matcher:
            profiles.append((matcher[PROFILE_KEY], matcher))
    if not profiles:
        return None
    if len(profiles) != 1 or manifest.get("category_key") != "model_3d_su":
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_scope_invalid")
    profile, matcher = profiles[0]
    validate_profile(profile)
    if mechanism_bindings(manifest) != profile["bindings"]:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_binding_mismatch")
    scale = resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": matcher})
    if not {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"} <= set(scale["enabled_levels"]):
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_level_scale_invalid")
    return profile, matcher


def probability(profile: Mapping, precheck: Mapping, dimensions: Mapping, scoring: Mapping) -> float:
    fixed, text = evidence_features(precheck, dimensions, scoring)
    text_entries = [(weight, idf) for name, weight, idf in profile["features"] if idf is not None and name in text]
    norm = math.sqrt(sum(idf * idf for _, idf in text_entries)) or 1
    linear = sum(weight * fixed.get(name, 0) for name, weight, idf in profile["features"] if idf is None)
    linear += sum(weight * 5 * idf / norm for weight, idf in text_entries)
    return 1 / (1 + math.exp(-max(-30, min(30, linear))))


def vote_aggregation(profile: Mapping) -> str:
    """profile 的多采样聚合方式；未配置即 ``mean``（与配置前逐字节同行为）。"""
    return profile.get("vote_aggregation", "mean")


def aggregate_probabilities(probabilities: Sequence[float], method: str) -> float:
    if method == "mean":
        return sum(probabilities) / len(probabilities)
    if method == "min":
        return min(probabilities)
    if method == "median":
        return statistics.median(probabilities)
    raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_vote_aggregation_invalid")


_VOTE_LABEL = {"mean": "均值", "min": "最小值", "median": "中位数"}


def can_calibrate(scoring: Mapping, dimensions: Mapping) -> bool:
    # Never promote a redline, a capped result, an unscored path or a filter.
    return not (not dimensions or scoring.get("hard_reject") or scoring.get("caps")
            or scoring.get("hard_defect_action") or scoring.get("needs_review")
            or scoring.get("level") not in {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"})


def dimension_view(dimensions: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """prompt_b 一份输出里校准会读的字段子集（多次采样时逐样本留证用）。

    与 ``evidence_features`` 读的键严格一致，因此 ``probability(view) == probability(full)``；
    其它模型字段不参与概率，也不进证据。非空输出至少保留 ``aesthetic_score`` 键，
    以免 ``b_present`` 特征因视图为空而翻转。
    """
    b = _mapping(dimensions)
    b = _mapping(b.get("dimension_output", b.get("dimension_result", b)))
    if not b:
        return {}
    view: dict[str, Any] = {"aesthetic_score": deepcopy(b.get("aesthetic_score"))}
    for key in ("overall_note", "aesthetic_evidence"):
        if key in b:
            view[key] = deepcopy(b[key])
    dims = _mapping(b.get("dimensions"))
    picked = {}
    for dim in DIMS:
        entry = _mapping(dims.get(dim))
        sub = {key: deepcopy(entry[key]) for key in ("hit_rules", "evaluation") if key in entry}
        if sub:
            picked[dim] = sub
    if picked:
        view["dimensions"] = picked
    return view


def apply_calibration(scoring: dict, *, profile: dict, matcher: dict,
                      precheck: Mapping, dimensions: Mapping,
                      vote_dimensions: Sequence[Mapping] | None = None) -> dict:
    """``vote_dimensions``：prompt_b 多次采样时每一份样本的输出（或其 ``dimension_view``）。

    给出两份以上时，概率按逐样本各算一次再按 profile 的 ``vote_aggregation`` 聚合
    （默认 ``mean``＝算术均值，候选 C1；``min``/``median`` 为 C1b）；``dimensions`` 仍是
    聚合后的代表输出，评分 ``scoring`` 仍是按中位数 base 分算出的那份。不给或只有
    一份时，行为与只看代表样本完全一致，结果不多任何键。

    证据 ``recommendation_calibration.vote``：``method`` 为 ``<聚合>_of_sample_probabilities``；
    ``mean`` 方式只记 ``mean``（与未配置时逐字节一致）；``min``/``median`` 方式记
    ``aggregated``（实际用于判定的聚合值）并同时保留 ``mean``（信息用，不参与判定）。
    """
    validate_profile(profile)
    if not can_calibrate(scoring, dimensions):
        return scoring
    score = scoring.get("score")
    if not _number(score, 0, 100):
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_score_invalid")
    scale = resolve_level_scale({"level_scale": matcher})
    thresholds = {entry["level"]: entry["min_score"] for entry in scale["thresholds"]}
    if not {"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4"} <= set(thresholds) or thresholds["L2"] < 1:
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_level_scale_invalid")
    samples = [s for s in (vote_dimensions or ()) if isinstance(s, Mapping)]
    vote: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    method = vote_aggregation(profile)
    if len(samples) >= 2:
        probabilities = [probability(profile, precheck, sample, scoring) for sample in samples]
        value = aggregate_probabilities(probabilities, method)
        vote = {"method": f"{method}_of_sample_probabilities", "samples": len(probabilities),
                "probabilities": probabilities, "mean": aggregate_probabilities(probabilities, "mean")}
        if method != "mean":
            vote["aggregated"] = value
        vote["representative_probability"] = probability(profile, precheck, dimensions, scoring)
    else:
        value = probability(profile, precheck, dimensions, scoring)
    recommended = value >= profile["threshold"]
    new_score = max(score, thresholds["L2"]) if recommended else min(score, thresholds["L2"] - 1)
    new_level = score_to_level(new_score, scale)
    if (new_level in POSITIVE) != recommended or new_level == "L5":
        raise ValueError("recommendation_calibration_level_inconsistent")
    result = deepcopy(scoring)
    result["recommendation_calibration"] = {
        "schema_version": SCHEMA, "profile_sha256": digest(profile),
        "base_engine_version": scoring.get("engine_version"),
        "original_score": score, "original_level": scoring["level"],
        "probability": value, "threshold": profile["threshold"],
        "recommended": recommended, "changed": new_level != scoring["level"],
        "training_purpose": profile["training"]["purpose"],
    }
    if vote is not None:
        result["recommendation_calibration"]["vote"] = vote
    result.update(engine_version=ENGINE_VERSION, score=new_score, level=new_level)
    result["steps"] = [*result.get("steps", []), {
        "step": "recommendation_calibration", "score_after": new_score,
        "note": f"视觉证据边界校准：{scoring['level']}（{score}分）→ {new_level}（{new_score}分）；"
                f"推荐概率{value:.4f}"
                + (f"（{vote['samples']}次采样逐样本概率{_VOTE_LABEL[method]}）" if vote is not None else "")
                + f"，冻结阈值{profile['threshold']:.4f}；原始模型与评分证据保留",
    }]
    return result

===== FILE backend/app/reconciliation_common.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import math
from collections.abc import Iterable, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import (
    Boolean,
    Column,
    DateTime,
    Float,
    Integer,
    LargeBinary,
    String,
)


class ReconciliationError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when source and target facts cannot be accepted as equivalent."""


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TableReconciliation:
    table_name: str
    source_row_count: int
    target_row_count: int
    source_primary_key_min: int | str | None
    source_primary_key_max: int | str | None
    target_primary_key_min: int | str | None
    target_primary_key_max: int | str | None
    source_null_counts: dict[str, int]
    target_null_counts: dict[str, int]
    source_hash: str
    target_hash: str

    @property
    def matches(self) -> bool:
        return (
            self.source_row_count == self.target_row_count
            and self.source_primary_key_min == self.target_primary_key_min
            and self.source_primary_key_max == self.target_primary_key_max
            and self.source_null_counts == self.target_null_counts
            and self.source_hash == self.target_hash
        )


CRITICAL_TABLES = frozenset(
    {
        "baseline_correction_runs",
        "baseline_regression_items",
        "baseline_regression_runs",
        "baseline_truth_revisions",
        "category_evaluation_v3_revisions",
        "evaluation_jobs",
        "evaluation_results",
        "label_outbox_events",
        "mechanism_releases",
        "production_runs",
        "production_step_attempts",
        "prompt_versions",
        "rule_module_versions",
        "semantic_tag_facts",
        "workflow_definitions",
        "workflow_versions",
    }
)


def _canonical_json(value: str, *, column_name: str) -> bytes:
    try:
        document = json.loads(value)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        raise ReconciliationError(
            f"字段 {column_name} 包含非法 JSON"
        ) from exc
    try:
        rendered = json.dumps(
            document,
            ensure_ascii=False,
            sort_keys=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            allow_nan=False,
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ReconciliationError(
            f"字段 {column_name} 无法规范化 JSON"
        ) from exc
    return rendered.encode("utf-8")


def _canonical_datetime(value: object, *, column_name: str) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(value, datetime):
        parsed = value
    elif isinstance(value, str):
        try:
            parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ReconciliationError(
                f"字段 {column_name} 包含非法时间"
            ) from exc
    else:
        raise ReconciliationError(f"字段 {column_name} 包含非法时间类型")
    if parsed.tzinfo is None:
        parsed = parsed.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    rendered = parsed.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat(
        timespec="microseconds"
    )
    return rendered.replace("+00:00", "Z").encode("ascii")


def _canonical_float(value: object, *, column_name: str) -> bytes:
    if isinstance(value, float) and not math.isfinite(value):
        raise ReconciliationError(f"字段 {column_name} 包含非有限浮点数")
    try:
        number = Decimal(str(value))
    except (InvalidOperation, ValueError) as exc:
        raise ReconciliationError(
            f"字段 {column_name} 包含非法浮点数"
        ) from exc
    if not number.is_finite():
        raise ReconciliationError(f"字段 {column_name} 包含非有限浮点数")
    if number == 0:
        return b"0"
    return format(number.normalize(), "f").encode("ascii")


def canonical_database_value(value: object, column: Column[Any]) -> bytes:
    """Return one dialect-independent, type-tagged database value."""

    if value is None:
        return b"N"
    if isinstance(column.type, Boolean):
        if type(value) not in (bool, int) or value not in (0, 1):
            raise ReconciliationError(
                f"字段 {column.name} 包含非法布尔值"
            )
        return b"B1" if bool(value) else b"B0"
    if isinstance(column.type, DateTime):
        return b"D" + _canonical_datetime(value, column_name=column.name)
    if isinstance(column.type, Float):
        return b"F" + _canonical_float(value, column_name=column.name)
    if isinstance(column.type, Integer):
        try:
            number = Decimal(str(value))
        except (InvalidOperation, ValueError) as exc:
            raise ReconciliationError(
                f"字段 {column.name} 包含非法整数"
            ) from exc
        if not number.is_finite() or number != number.to_integral_value():
            raise ReconciliationError(f"字段 {column.name} 包含非法整数")
        return b"I" + str(int(number)).encode("ascii")
    if isinstance(column.type, LargeBinary):
        if not isinstance(value, (bytes, bytearray, memoryview)):
            raise ReconciliationError(
                f"字段 {column.name} 包含非法二进制值"
            )
        return b"Y" + bytes(value)
    if isinstance(column.type, String):
        rendered = str(value)
        if column.name.endswith("_json"):
            return b"J" + _canonical_json(
                rendered,
                column_name=column.name,
            )
        return b"S" + rendered.encode("utf-8")
    raise ReconciliationError(
        f"字段 {column.name} 使用未支持类型 {type(column.type).__name__}"
    )


def table_digest(
    rows: Iterable[Mapping[str, Any]],
    *,
    table_name: str,
    columns: Sequence[Column[Any]],
    primary_key_name: str,
) -> tuple[int, int | str | None, int | str | None, dict[str, int], str]:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    digest.update(b"labelsystem-table-reconciliation-v1\0")
    digest.update(table_name.encode("utf-8"))
    null_counts = {column.name: 0 for column in columns}
    row_count = 0
    primary_key_min: int | str | None = None
    primary_key_max: int | str | None = None
    for row in rows:
        primary_key = row[primary_key_name]
        if row_count == 0:
            primary_key_min = primary_key
        primary_key_max = primary_key
        row_count += 1
        digest.update(b"\0row\0")
        for column in columns:
            value = row[column.name]
            if value is None:
                null_counts[column.name] += 1
            canonical = canonical_database_value(value, column)
            name = column.name.encode("utf-8")
            digest.update(len(name).to_bytes(4, "big"))
            digest.update(name)
            digest.update(len(canonical).to_bytes(8, "big"))
            digest.update(canonical)
    return (
        row_count,
        primary_key_min,
        primary_key_max,
        null_counts,
        digest.hexdigest(),
    )


def schema_contract_compatible(
    *,
    source_columns: list[str],
    target_columns: list[str],
    source_primary_key: list[str],
    target_primary_key: list[str],
) -> bool:
    """Compare relational schema semantics, not physical column order."""

    return (
        len(source_columns) == len(target_columns)
        and set(source_columns) == set(target_columns)
        and source_primary_key == target_primary_key
    )

===== FILE backend/app/redline_policy.py =====
"""Deterministic redline pre-filter for ADR-0033 (framework-first phase).

This module is a **pure-function** contract layer: it performs no IO, no
network calls, no database access and no model calls.  Every function is
referentially transparent and produces JSON-serializable, order-stable output
for a given input, so the redline decision is fully regressible.

Scope for this phase (framework only):
- Freeze the ``redline-policy-v1`` data contract and its fail-closed validator.
- Evaluate redlines against an already-produced 调用A precheck payload.

Out of scope (later independent phases): wiring into the worker/production
path, the L-level direction migration, and touching published/consumer
contracts.  The ``hit_level`` value here is validated for legality only
(``L1``..``L5``); its "淘汰档" direction semantics are handled elsewhere.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Any

REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION = "redline-policy-v1"

_RULE_KEY_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{2,39}$")
_VALID_HIT_LEVELS = frozenset({"L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"})
_SUPPORTED_SIGNAL = "production_fields.reason"


class RedlinePolicyError(ValueError):
    """Raised when a redline policy is structurally or semantically invalid.

    Carries a stable ``code`` so callers can branch on the failure class
    without string matching the (localized) message.
    """

    def __init__(self, code: str, message: str) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.code = code


def _is_int(value: Any) -> bool:
    return isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool)


def validate_redline_policy(policy: Any) -> None:
    """Fail-closed structural/enum/uniqueness validation of a redline policy.

    Raises ``RedlinePolicyError`` (a ``ValueError`` subclass with ``.code``)
    on any violation. Business values are owned by the frozen versioned
    contract; this validator only enforces their executable-safe shape.
    """
    if not isinstance(policy, dict):
        raise RedlinePolicyError("policy_not_object", "红线策略必须是对象")
    if policy.get("format_version") != REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION:
        raise RedlinePolicyError(
            "format_version_unsupported",
            f"红线策略版本必须是 {REDLINE_POLICY_FORMAT_VERSION}",
        )

    enabled = policy.get("enabled")
    if not isinstance(enabled, bool):
        raise RedlinePolicyError("enabled_not_bool", "红线策略 enabled 必须是布尔值")

    if policy.get("hit_level") not in _VALID_HIT_LEVELS:
        raise RedlinePolicyError(
            "hit_level_invalid", "红线 hit_level 必须是 L1 至 L5 之一"
        )

    hit_score_cap = policy.get("hit_score_cap")
    if not _is_int(hit_score_cap) or not 0 <= hit_score_cap <= 100:
        raise RedlinePolicyError(
            "hit_score_cap_out_of_range", "红线 hit_score_cap 必须是 0 至 100 的整数"
        )

    rules = policy.get("rules")
    if not isinstance(rules, list):
        raise RedlinePolicyError("rules_not_list", "红线 rules 必须是数组")
    # Redline rules can be freely added/removed/toggled with no fixed count.
    # An enabled redline stage with zero (or all-disabled) rules is legal: it
    # simply never hits — parallel to the dimensions relaxation.

    seen_keys: set[str] = set()
    for rule in rules:
        if not isinstance(rule, dict):
            raise RedlinePolicyError("rule_not_object", "红线规则必须是对象")

        key = rule.get("key")
        if not isinstance(key, str) or not _RULE_KEY_PATTERN.match(key):
            raise RedlinePolicyError("rule_key_invalid", "红线规则 key 不符合命名规范")
        if key in seen_keys:
            raise RedlinePolicyError("rule_key_duplicate", f"红线规则 key 重复：{key}")
        seen_keys.add(key)

        rule_enabled = rule.get("enabled", True)
        if not isinstance(rule_enabled, bool):
            raise RedlinePolicyError(
                "rule_enabled_invalid", f"红线规则 {key} 的 enabled 必须是布尔值"
            )

        if rule.get("signal") != _SUPPORTED_SIGNAL:
            raise RedlinePolicyError(
                "signal_unsupported",
                f"本阶段红线只支持信号源 {_SUPPORTED_SIGNAL}",
            )

        match_any = rule.get("match_any")
        if not isinstance(match_any, list) or not match_any:
            raise RedlinePolicyError(
                "match_any_empty", "红线规则 match_any 必须是非空数组"
            )
        for value in match_any:
            if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.strip():
                raise RedlinePolicyError(
                    "match_value_invalid", "红线 match_any 必须是非空业务值字符串"
                )

        exemptions = rule.get("exemptions", [])
        if not isinstance(exemptions, list) or any(
            not isinstance(item, str) or not item for item in exemptions
        ):
            raise RedlinePolicyError(
                "exemptions_invalid", "红线规则 exemptions 必须是非空字符串数组或空数组"
            )
        exemption_flag = rule.get("exemption_flag")
        if exemption_flag is not None and (
            not isinstance(exemption_flag, str)
            or not _RULE_KEY_PATTERN.fullmatch(exemption_flag)
        ):
            raise RedlinePolicyError("exemption_flag_invalid", "红线豁免位必须是有效的事实字段名")

        required_defects = rule.get("requires_any_hard_defect")
        if required_defects is not None and (
            not isinstance(required_defects, list)
            or not required_defects
            or len(required_defects) != len(set(required_defects))
            or any(
                not isinstance(item, str)
                or not item.strip()
                for item in required_defects
            )
        ):
            raise RedlinePolicyError(
                "requires_any_hard_defect_invalid",
                "红线规则 requires_any_hard_defect 必须是非空且去重的业务值字符串数组",
            )


def evaluate_redlines(precheck: Any, *, policy: dict) -> dict:
    """Deterministically decide whether a precheck payload hits any redline.

    Validates ``policy`` first (fail-closed).  When the policy is disabled the
    function short-circuits to a non-eliminating result.  Individual rules may
    be toggled off via a per-rule ``enabled: false`` and are skipped.  Matching
    reads only ``precheck["production_fields"]["reason"]`` (missing / non-list ->
    empty).  A rule matches when its ``match_any`` intersects the reason list;
    if that rule carries ``exemptions`` and any exemption text also appears in
    the reason list, the rule is exempted and does not count as a hit.

    Output is a fixed-shape, JSON-serializable, input-stable dict.
    """
    validate_redline_policy(policy)

    if not policy["enabled"]:
        return {"hit": False, "hit_rules": [], "hard_reject": False}

    reasons: set[str] = set()
    production_fields = precheck.get("production_fields") if isinstance(precheck, dict) else None
    if isinstance(production_fields, dict):
        raw_reason = production_fields.get("reason")
        if isinstance(raw_reason, list):
            reasons = {item for item in raw_reason if isinstance(item, str)}
    hard_defects: set[str] = set()
    if isinstance(precheck, dict):
        raw_hard_defects = precheck.get("hard_defects")
        if isinstance(raw_hard_defects, list):
            hard_defects = {
                item for item in raw_hard_defects if isinstance(item, str)
            }

    hit_rules: list[str] = []
    for rule in policy["rules"]:
        if not rule.get("enabled", True):
            continue
        if not reasons.intersection(rule["match_any"]):
            continue
        # Only a flag explicitly named by this frozen rule can exempt it.
        # Missing, string or numeric flags never grant an exemption.
        exemption_flags = precheck.get("redline_exempted") if isinstance(precheck, dict) else None
        if (isinstance(exemption_flags, dict)
                and rule.get("exemption_flag")
                and exemption_flags.get(rule["exemption_flag"]) is True):
            continue
        exemptions = rule.get("exemptions", [])
        if any(exemption in reasons for exemption in exemptions):
            continue
        required_defects = rule.get("requires_any_hard_defect")
        if required_defects and not hard_defects.intersection(
            required_defects
        ):
            continue
        key = rule["key"]
        if key not in hit_rules:
            hit_rules.append(key)

    if not hit_rules:
        return {"hit": False, "hit_rules": [], "hard_reject": False}

    return {
        "hit": True,
        "hit_rules": hit_rules,
        "hit_level": policy["hit_level"],
        "hit_score_cap": policy["hit_score_cap"],
        "hard_reject": True,
    }
